World totals are linted per legal loaded set (DA-32): `{home}` 8, `{home, A}` 14, `{home, B}` 15, `{home+B}` 15, `{home+B, A}` 21, all at or below 28. Home LOAD nodes number 11 (7 lamps, the beacon lamp `bcn_main`, two door motors, one heater) against LS-26's "about 8 LOADs"; under the one-sided tolerance of Formula D.2 that passes (over by 3, tolerance `max(floor(8 x 20 / 100), 3)` = 3; the lint warns only when over exceeds the tolerance, so 3 passes with no margin). Every room can be re-lit and un-lit (`fixtures_min` 1; the player switches a lamp with the Emitter's `set_switch`, whose PLAYER-origin form toggles against the true `switch_on`, IA Interactions row), which is what makes restraint (P3) possible.

**DA-23 The EMERGENCY lamp and the wake light.** One FIXTURE `fx_cryo_lamp` in `cryo_bay` with `node_ref` 0 (self-powered), `fixture_subkind_emergency` (`#FFCC66`), holding shadow slot 6 (`SHADOW_SLOTS_MAX` 6 `[PLACEHOLDER: Lighting]`), never modulated by Attention's tell (LO-20); it is the only `node_ref` 0 fixture of subkind EMERGENCY in the world (`SELF_POWERED_LAMP_COUNT` counts the EMERGENCY subkind only, EM-11, EM-24); the three dock status glows of DA-46 are a separate, ruled self-powered class that the Emitter must name (an EM-3 / EM-11 amendment is owed, `[PLACEHOLDER: Emitter]`) and are counted by `DOCK_GLOW_COUNT`, never by `SELF_POWERED_LAMP_COUNT` *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*. There is **no cryo-pod interior light in MVP**: the cryo pod carries no light; `fx_cryo_lamp` is the bay's only EMERGENCY light. Lighting Ask E-H and OQ 6 are answered "none"; ART 2.2's "pod interior light dies within the first minute" is routed to the art-director as a Vertical Slice beat, and any later pod light is a real FIXTURE placement (a subkind and a `base_pm`) whose `output_pm` falls in the simulation, never a Lighting-only glow (LO:451), and it never takes shadow slot 6 from `fx_cryo_lamp`. Lint `WAKE_SINGLE_LIGHT` (ERROR, CI): at step 0 the only lit emitter of kind FIXTURE, NODE_LANTERN or BEACON in the `cryo_bay` compartment is `fx_cryo_lamp`, and the beacon is dark; FAULT_GLOW rows are counted by the glow lints (DA-40), not here. Under the shipping staging no FAULT_GLOW row is active in the cryo bay at step 0 either (`p_cryo_rad` is a coolant LEAK with no glow row, EM-17, and O2-SHIP is silent behind its closed valve); under the retired valve-OPEN contingency (DA-16) the O2-SHIP `1/GAS` row would be the one permitted exception and this scope keeps the lint green on it *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*. (orchestrator ruling: the art-director read ART 2.2's "darkest state in the game" as the whole ship dark at wake; R8 authors the other home fixtures `switch_on` true with only the cryo branch unserved, and a reactor running at wake serves them. The lint is scoped to the cryo-bay compartment, the room the player wakes in; Checkpoint rulings Q2 confirmed the scope: ART 2.2 binds the wake room, ART 2.4's "dark" is a different state, and lit adjoining rooms behind a closed door do not break the wake. A one-line ART 2.2/2.4 note is routed to the art-director, non-blocking.)

**DA-24 Lanterns and the beacon.** *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)* Lanterns are NODE_LANTERNs derived from a *running* node (EM-14), one per machine node whose def carries an `emits` with shape `LANTERN` (IDB-31, EM-14 as restated 2026-10-28: `mod_reactor_cell`, `mod_coolant_pump`, `mod_scrubber`): `home_ship` 3 (`r_main`, `pump_main`, `scr_main`), `derelict_a` 2 (`pump_a`, `scr_a`), `derelict_b` 3 (`r_b`, `pump_b`, `scr_b`); world 8 against a cap of 20, and at most `LANTERN_LIGHTS_MAX` 8 `[PLACEHOLDER: Lighting]` in view (LO-23, a subset of `NONSHADOW_LIGHTS_MAX` 24 `[PLACEHOLDER: Lighting]`). A lantern on a dead or SCRAMmed machine is dark (the failed scrubber never lights; `r_b` lights only after V12), which is also the truth witness for the pump stall. **Worst-case view against `LANTERN_LIGHTS_MAX` 8** *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)*: authored lanterns per loaded set are S0 {home} 3; S1 {home, A} 3 + 2 = 5; S2 {home, B} and S3 {home+B} 3 + 3 = 6; S4 {home+B, A} 3 + 3 + 2 = 8. The largest set equals `LANTERN_LIGHTS_MAX` 8 at tiers T0 and T1 (Lighting caps 8 / 8 / 6 / 4 by tier), so no authored view can exceed the cap at T0 or T1, and at T2 (6) and T3 (4) the excess are demoted to stand-ins and never dropped (LO-23); the worst single home-ship view is 3 (home alone) or 6 (welded home+B). The world count 8 is below the cap of 20, and the emitter tally for S4 is unchanged (D.3: 21 fixtures + 8 lanterns + 1 beacon + at most 9 glow rows = 39 authored). This amendment adds no lantern: the 3 / 2 / 3 budget and the registry's `mvp_fixture_counts` stand. A scrubber installed into a vacant slot after `init_world` is not counted here (OQ-DA-55). There is **exactly one BEACON** in the world data, on `home_ship` in `spine_corridor`, with line of sight to `door_cryo_s`, `gates_actions` false, authored `beacon_phase` and `beacon_dir` (the Emitter's units; the design intent is a sweep that crosses the cryo-bay door) and `node_ref` = `bcn_main`, a **dedicated LOAD node** (`mod_lamp`, 20 W) with its own hardpoint. (Closes OQ-DA-9 as "no sharing": the Emitter's state table makes `set_beacon` off issue M14 `set_load_request(node_ref, 0)`, which on a node shared with `lamp_corr_2` would zero that fixture's draw while `fx_corr_2` still shines on its tier's `served_pm` (EM-11), a lamp lit for free and a P1 lie. The beacon is SYSTEM-switched, so it also shifts PWR-OVL's trigger rho; D.9 holds the window with the beacon off and on.) `BEACON_SWEEP_TICKS` `[PLACEHOLDER: Emitter]` is 128. Attention (designed, AT-18) submits `set_beacon` (SYSTEM only) to the one beacon the data holds; the placement is this GDD's data row, and Attention neither moves nor adds a beacon. Zero beacons on A and B. `BEACON_COUNT` (ERROR, BOOT).

**DA-25 The part ledger, the spare breaker and G-0.** (registry: `mvp_part_ledger`) The Item DB's advisory band is the capped aggregate `part_cover_pm(R) = floor(1000 x sum_f min(supply(f, R), demand(f, R)) / sum_f demand(f, R))` (per part family f, `floor(1000 x supply(f, R) / demand(f, R))`, with `PART_COVER_UNDEFINED` -1 when demand is 0 and the warning `PART_TYPE_UNSUPPLIED` when demand is above 0 and supply is 0) within `PART_COVER_MIN_PM` 500 to `PART_COVER_MAX_PM` 800 (IDB F-11; cited, not minted here). Demand is **5 part-taking FULL-repairable authored faults** (IDB F-11 scope, not 7): the pinhole DUCT `d_cryo_exposed` (duct 1), the overloaded CABLE `cab_trunk` (cable 1), the coolant PIPE `p_cryo_rad` and the fuel PIPE `pf_feed` (pipe 2) and the fatigued HULL_SEGMENT `seg_b_pump_n` (hull 1). Excluded and logged: the O2 seam (`repair_part_for` NONE), the broken CABLE_JUNCTION (covered in kind by the spare breaker), the pump stall (STARVE, takes no part) and the SCRAM (cleared by a reset). Supply is **4 loose parts: A 2, B 2**, none on `home_ship` (duct 1, cable 1, pipe 1, hull 1, kit 0): at S4 part_cover_pm = floor(1000 x (1 + 1 + 1 + 1) / 5) = floor(4000 / 5) = **800 permille**, inside the band at the top edge. Per family: duct 1000, cable 1000, hull 1000, pipe floor(1000 x 1 / 2) = 500, `part_machine_kit` `PART_COVER_UNDEFINED` (-1, 0 of 0, no warning). *(Amended 2026-10-27 — consistency pass after design review)* *(Amended 2026-10-28 — delegated ruling G2, creative-director lean; was 5 loose parts, 714 permille on the uncapped aggregate over 7 demands)*

| Part | Placed on (slot) | Repairs (family) | Reached by |
|---|---|---|---|
| `part_hull_plate` (the 30 kg object, SE:73) | A, `a_spine` floor | fatigued HULL_SEGMENT `seg_b_pump_n` | first sortie; a heavy stowed part (STOWED, stack_max 1, HEAVY cue, SE-28; **while any HEAVY item is stowed, run is disabled**, user ruling D18 (2026-10-27): the carrier moves at `V_WALK` 2, and `V_CARRY` and the carry noise stay locked) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* |
| `part_cable_splice` | A, `a_machine` floor | overloaded trunk CABLE | first sortie, inside the lethal cold room |
| `part_pipe_fitting` x 1 | B, `b_pump` floor | ONE of coolant PIPE `p_cryo_rad` or fuel PIPE `pf_feed`, the player's choice | second sortie *(Amended 2026-10-28 — delegated ruling G2, creative-director lean)* |
| `part_duct_coupling` | B, `b_breach` floor | exposed DUCT `d_cryo_exposed` | second sortie, across the vacuum |

Family cover: duct 1 of 1, cable 1 of 1, hull 1 of 1, pipe 1 of 2. Uncovered by choice (logged): the O2 seam (a SEAM edge whose full-repair capability is itself routed, IDB-20) and the broken junction (covered in kind by the spare breaker). No `part_machine_kit` is placed (a full repair of the salvaged scrubber is a Vertical Slice question, OQ-DA-12). **One spare `mod_breaker_junction`** (`condition_bp` 10000) sits on A in `a_life`: a module, not part supply (IDB OQ 19, a recommendation), and it is the fix for fault #5. **No `curio_data_core`** is placed in MVP. **G-0:** the MVP is always survivable with zero parts: every home-ship live fault is patchable to `PATCH_CAP_BP` 6000 and re-patched as the patch decays (the treadmill), and A's broken junction at `hp` 2000 reaches `PATCH_CAP_BP` by patch (IDB AC-42; headless test `G0_ZERO_PART_SURVIVABLE`). Access order matters for pacing, and is deliberate scarcity (P5): the first fault of the game, the cryo-bay coolant pipe, cannot be fully repaired until the second sortie (about 45 to 55 minutes in), so a player carries about 20 patch actions on the leaky home faults before their first full repair. **Guarded by a playtest target, not retuned (CD-GDD-ALIGN concern, applied):** the MVP core hypothesis is that a 30+ minute solo session is tense and absorbing, and a treadmill of about 20 patches on a 7.5-minute leak cycle across up to three home faults (CLT-LEAK, FUEL-DRIP and, once the player opens `v_o2_cryo`, O2-SHIP) can turn tension into upkeep. The ledger and the scarcity stay exactly as authored until the playtest; target **X-DA-9** (Game Feel; AC-DA-136) measures it, and if it fails the named levers are `PATCH_DECAY_STEPS` (a Graph value, LSG D.9, so a routed ask to the Graph owner and not a local edit) and the home leak count (3; a content edit here, with D.15 and AC-DA-22 re-run). A DESTROYED module node yields one part (IDB-29), so part instances during play may exceed 4; `PART_SUPPLY_EXCEEDS` bounds placements statically and the headless run counts instances. **Cover on repairable demand and the minting bound** *(Amended 2026-10-28 — delegated ruling G2, creative-director lean)*: the ledger was tightened on 2026-10-28 (OQ-DA-52 resolved): with demand counted on the five part-taking faults and the owner's capped aggregate, the cover is 800 permille at S4, a real placement statistic and not an artefact of unmeetable demands. A destroyed module yields at most one part (IDB-29), so `PART_INSTANCES_MAX` = 4 placed + 66 module nodes x 1 = **70** part instances in a world, the bound the headless run asserts (a ceiling, not a target; if the Item DB's yield per destroyed node (IDB-29) rises above 1, the bound is 4 + 66 x that yield). The player who welds B before carrying the A hull plate to `b_pump` lives with a 3.5-minute patch treadmill on `seg_b_pump_n` for the rest of the session (D.15, test `WELD_WITHOUT_PLATE_SURVIVABLE`); the GDD states the dependency plainly rather than gating it. **Where the scarcity actually lives (design review 2026-10-27; amended 2026-10-28).** There are four parts for five part-repairable faults. The four non-pipe parts still map one-to-one onto duct, cable, hull and one pipe, and the pipe family is the one place the count bites: ONE fitting, TWO pipe faults, so the player chooses whether the cryo-bay coolant pipe or the fuel-drip pipe gets the permanent fix and the other stays on the patch treadmill (G-0 untouched: every home fault is still patchable with zero parts). The rest of the pressure stays order and time: the access order (the first fault's full repair waits for the second sortie), the loaded-time clocks that run while the player is aboard (the hull-fatigue breach at 5.0 minutes, the bus battery, the residual lamp) and the 30 kg plate that cannot be hurried. No trip or carry limit is assumed here; if Web Map & Transit or Boarding & Salvage publishes one (a pod carry capacity, a per-sortie cap) it is theirs to name and this paragraph then cites it (routed, OQ-DA-42). The 800 permille sits at the top edge of the Item DB band as a placement statistic, and it is also a real choice: one pipe fitting for two pipe faults *(Amended 2026-10-28 — delegated ruling G2, creative-director lean)*. One degenerate loop is noted: a DESTROYED module node yields `scrap_count` parts (IDB-29), so deliberately wrecking a salvageable module can mint a part; the supply is bounded by the node list (each node once, nothing respawns), `PART_SUPPLY_EXCEEDS` bounds placements, and the headless run counts instances (AC-DA-30) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*.

**DA-26 Loose placements.** Authoring supplies `{def_id, count, condition_bp}` at Layout's `loose_slots(section_id)` anchors (LY-22; Layout reserves no item table) *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix: Item DB IDB-15, IA-28, G1/G6)*. `count` is 1 for modules, parts and curios and the stack for glowsticks; `condition_bp` is 1..10000 for a module and 0 for every other def. `cap_bp`, `patched_at_step` and `charge` are NEVER authored. At world build the loader expands each placement with the Item DB's pure `fresh_instance(def_id, count, condition_bp)` (IDB-28), which sets `cap_bp` to `HP_FULL` (10000) for a module and 0 otherwise, `patched_at_step` 0 and `charge` 0, and Interaction's `place_loose(inst, section_id, pos_px)` receives the instance (the six-field array `[def_id, count, condition_bp, cap_bp, patched_at_step, charge]` is shown per slot in the table). A seeded derelict module is therefore un-patched; patch history exists only on installed nodes and on instances the player cut. Interaction's `place_loose` consumes the expanded instances at world build in the order `build_section` then Graph M1 then Emitter E1 then Interaction `sync_section` (IA-28). Only parts, the spare breaker and `cons_glowstick` stacks (each at most `GLOWSTICK_STACK_MAX` 4 `[PLACEHOLDER: Item DB]`) are placed; no placement duplicates the Suit start kit (IDB AC-60: a tool already held loose is a warning); no weapon, no enemy.

| Section | Slot (`slot_id`: room, surface) | `def_id` | count / condition_bp, and the expanded instance | Purpose |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| home | `ls_cargo_crate`: `cargo_bay`, CRATE | `cons_glowstick` | 4 / 0: `['cons_glowstick',4,0,0,0,0]` | the first pickup |
| home | `ls_air_locker`: `airlock`, SHELF | `cons_glowstick` | 4 / 0: `['cons_glowstick',4,0,0,0,0]` | pod-hop supply |
| A | `ls_a_life_shelf`: `a_life`, SHELF | `mod_breaker_junction` | 1 / 10000: `['mod_breaker_junction',1,10000,10000,0,0]` | the spare breaker |
| A | `ls_a_machine_floor`: `a_machine`, FLOOR | `part_cable_splice` | 1 / 0: `['part_cable_splice',1,0,0,0,0]` | inside the cold room |
| A | `ls_a_spine_floor`: `a_spine`, FLOOR | `part_hull_plate` | 1 / 0: `['part_hull_plate',1,0,0,0,0]` (30 kg) | the heavy stowed part |
| A | `ls_a_dock_shelf`: `a_dock`, SHELF | `cons_glowstick` | 2 / 0: `['cons_glowstick',2,0,0,0,0]` | |
| B | `ls_b_pump_floor`: `b_pump`, FLOOR | `part_pipe_fitting` | 1 / 0: `['part_pipe_fitting',1,0,0,0,0]` | in the fatigue room; the one fitting for two pipe faults *(Amended 2026-10-28 — delegated ruling G2, creative-director lean)* |
| B | `ls_b_breach_floor`: `b_breach`, FLOOR | `part_duct_coupling` | 1 / 0: `['part_duct_coupling',1,0,0,0,0]` | across the vacuum |
| B | `ls_b_dock_shelf`: `b_dock`, SHELF | `cons_glowstick` | 2 / 0: `['cons_glowstick',2,0,0,0,0]` | |

Twelve glowsticks are placed in total across the nine loose placements (stacks 4, 4, 2 and 2; `GLOWSTICK_PLACED`; the carried start count is Suit's); `PLACEMENT_BAD_INSTANCE` (DA-40) fires when `fresh_instance` returns a reason code (a non-zero `condition_bp` on a non-module, a `count` above `stack_max`, a `condition_bp` outside 1..10000 on a module) *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix: Item DB IDB-15, IA-28, G1/G6)*. No loose slot sits within 1 tile of a fault's maximum floor run (`LOOSE_ON_TRAIL_RUN`); a slot never sits on a door (Layout's `LOOSE_ON_DOOR`, cited). Seeded loose items per set: `{home, A}` 6, `{home, B}` 5, `{home+B, A}` 9 *(Amended 2026-10-28 — delegated ruling G2, creative-director lean)*.

**DA-27 The one visible unknown per wreck.** Each DERELICT template carries exactly one FIXTURE flagged `residual: true`: a `mod_lamp` LOAD (`req_w` 20 W) with `switch_on` true, on a **residual island** whose only source is one `mod_battery_cell` at authored partial charge, tied to the wreck's dead or scrammed bus by a junction that is OPEN at build (the island is closed: one LOAD, one source, one tie, `RESIDUAL_LEDGER_CLOSED`). The template carries the record `visible_unknown {emitter local_id, room_key, exterior_anchor_px, hue_class}`, read by Web Map & Transit for the exterior transit view, whose lit test is `residual_lamp_predicate` (Web Map & Transit WM-18: lit iff the lamp node is OK or DEGRADED and the battery stock is > 0); Authoring draws nothing and puts no marker on it.

| Wreck | Lamp (`local_id`, `node_ref`) | Host room | Battery, charge | Tie | `exterior_anchor_px` (on the hull run, design target) | `hue_class` |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| A | `fx_a_res`, `lamp_a_life` | `a_life` | `b_a1`, `stock` 540,000 dJ (15 % of `BATT_CELL` 3,600,000 dJ) | `j_a_res` OPEN | (576, 84) on the north hull run | Tungsten (`#FFCC66`) |
| B | `fx_b_res`, `lamp_b_breach` | `b_breach` | `b_b1`, `stock` 432,000 dJ (12 % of `BATT_CELL`) | `j_b_res` OPEN | (948, 672) on the east hull run | Tungsten |

Clock (Formula D.6): a 20 W lamp draws 20 dJ per Graph step (200 dJ/s), so A's lamp burns 27,000 steps = **45 minutes of loaded time** and B's 21,600 steps = **36 minutes**. The clock counts *loaded* time only, because load-dormancy (DA-13 (a)) freezes an unloaded derelict with no fast-forward: the light cannot drain while the player is away, and a lamp found dark on a later visit is the honest reading of the player's own time aboard (the exterior view shows a built wreck's residual lamp lit iff its lamp node state is OK or DEGRADED and its persisted battery stock is > 0 (`residual_lamp_predicate`, Web Map and Transit WM-18), and a never-built wreck lit if `charge` > 0). (orchestrator ruling: R7's worked example of 25 % and 75 min outlives two 30-minute sessions against its own "not two sessions", and the load-dormancy rule it cites makes the example moot; 15 % and 12 % on loaded time are adopted on the level-designer's, systems-designer's and creative-director's agreement, conditional on the DRAINED lamp reading as honest and on Attention not modulating a residual lamp.) The lamp is steady and unmodulated (the gutter is the cryo lamp's grammar; the exterior unknown must be a fixed honest point). Any withdrawal from the island battery (a Suit cell top-up) shortens the lamp step for step. Per user ruling X5 (2026-10-27) the Suit's top-up is a deliberate MVP verb (the lamp cell and the go-dark lever exist at MVP), so this withdrawal is a live MVP act and not a contingency: topping up from `b_a1` or `b_b1` is the player spending the wreck's visible unknown on their own beam, and D.6's `withdrawn_dJ` is a shipping input *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*. A closed tie on a powered bus that has a source lets the bus recharge the battery (a V3 decision; B only: A has no source, so on A a closed tie merges the two batteries into one pool, D.16, and ends the lamp's own 45-minute clock) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*. B's SCRAM rim glow is an additional glow and not the residual light. Host-room rules (VA-6, lints `VISIBLE_UNKNOWN_NO_PORT`, `VISIBLE_UNKNOWN_COMPETITOR`): the host room's hull-run wall carries a `wall_port` piece within 48 px of `exterior_anchor_px` (a port rect that is SOLID but not `OCCLUDES_LIGHT`, a Layout ask, OQ-DA-19), and the host room has no other lit FIXTURE, NODE_LANTERN or glow-row fault. Lint `RESIDUAL_LIGHT_COUNT` (ERROR, BOOT): exactly one per DERELICT template, zero per HOME, on a battery-served island with `charge` > 0. Rejected: FAULT_GLOW as the unknown (its lifetime is `DRIED_AFTER_STEPS`'s, not the author's); a second self-powered subkind (breaks EM-11 and AC-58 and P1). That rejection concerns the visible unknown: the dock status glow of DA-46 is a different, ruled class with a different job (the hop-availability guarantee, BS-19) and a luminance ceiling below the residual lamp's, and it never appears in a wreck's exterior view *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*. The note owed to the Item DB and Graph owners: B carries one `mod_battery_cell` although IDB:237 contrasts "derelict B a live REACTOR"; no value changes.

#### F. Hazard rooms, fairness, budgets and truth

**DA-28 Hazard rooms: the one lethal cold room, the vacuum traverse, B's breach.**

- *Lethal cold (derelict A only).* `a_machine` is authored at `T0` -22000 mC with `forced_mode` FREEZE on its liquid pipes (LSG:764: a derelict "already frozen" is a content fact, not an endogenous transition). The shifted cold onset is -20000 mC (`COLD_DMG_ONSET_MC` -15000 less `INSUL_MC` 5000); the room's equilibrium sits at the plate-term stall, `ceil(100000 / (K_HULL x exposed_tiles))` = ceil(100000 / 325) = 308 mC above `T_AMB_MC` -30000 (about -29692 mC; the Graph's 182 mC is its 22-tile example, not this room), far below the onset, which satisfies Suit's export (SE-21: a lethal cold room must have equilibrium below the shifted onset) `[derived; Graph owner to confirm with LSG D.7, OQ-DA-4]`. Suit's export is why the rule exists: with `INSUL_MC` 5000 an unpowered home-ship needs about 40 minutes to reach the shifted onset (SE-21), so a cold-hazard derelict room must be authored with an equilibrium temperature below the shifted cold onset or it is no hazard. It is the only lethal-cold room in MVP (B's hazard is heat and vacuum; B's heat room is `b_reactor`, next bullet). It is entered from `a_spine` (`T0` -12000 mC) through `door_a_spine_machine`, with a dried frost fan creeping out over the door sill, visible in the beam: the tell precedes the hazard (P2). Crossing is safe and lingering is lethal: at `T0` the room reaches CRITICAL cold in about 36 s and FAILED in about 109 s (Suit's D.4 with the Suit knobs; Formula D.10), so a seven-tile crossing takes 5.6 s carrying while the patch channel inside is the lethal act. The room's heater `htr_a` and the bus battery `b_a2` sit on the branch that the spare breaker wakes. **Feeding the wreck has one computed benefit** (D.22): the heater lifts the room from -22000 mC above the shifted onset -20000 mC after 447 steps (45 s) and holds it out of damaging cold for about 12.9 minutes on the full bus (`b_a2` 1,620,000 dJ), after which it returns to damaging cold, so it is a bus battery's lifetime and not a base. **User ruling (D45, 2026-10-27): this computed window is the whole payoff of waking the branch for the first build, deliberately modest; no powered door, lamps-lit-only fact or second live tell is authored. The X-DA-8 reasons log (AC-DA-135) is the trigger for any later authored payoff, which the creative-director then owns (OQ-DA-43 options B to D)** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*. In-room work is budgeted by `t_work_max` (D.10): 25.2 s at `T0`, 13.8 s after 30 minutes unheated, 6.2 s at the equilibrium, module carried both ways. The junction `j_a_main` sits in `a_machine`: the swap is two channels (about 8 s) plus a 5.6 s crossing, 14 of the 25.2 s at `T0`, and the room needs 447 steps (44.7 s) after the breaker closes to pass the onset, so staying through them accrues about 4,200 of exposure against `TL_CRITICAL` 4,000 (gain 11 falling to 8); the order is swap, close the breaker, step back into `a_spine` for about 45 s and return (whether exposure recovers once the room is above the onset is Suit D.4's, cited and not assumed) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*. The order is safe without any recovery *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*: the 14 s inside accrue at most 140 runs x gain 11 = 1,540, `a_spine` (`T0` -12000 mC) is above the onset and accrues nothing, and on return the room is above the onset too, so the peak is at most 1,540 against `TL_CRITICAL` 4,000 even if exposure never falls; AC-DA-153 checks it on a Suit stub. The quiet alternative (cut `scr_a` in `a_life` and leave) never enters the room, and the crossing at the authored speed is safe either way (D.10) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*. `COLD_ROOM_UNDECLARED` (ERROR): a room whose `T0` is below the shifted onset must be declared lethal, and exactly one per MVP body may be. The "exactly one lethal room" invariant is a *build-state* invariant: opening the door between `a_spine` and `a_machine` mixes them by conduction, so the lint also prints the open-door projection.
- *Heat (derelict B, `b_reactor`, after V12; `[derived; Graph owner to confirm Q_src_room]`).* `b_reactor` is authored at `T0` 42000 mC (the HOT band, 40000 and up, LSG:96), 3000 mC under `HEAT_DMG_ONSET_MC` 45000 (registry). Suit's heat axis has no insulation shift (`e_heat` = `T` - `HEAT_DMG_ONSET_MC`, SE D.4), so the room is harmless as authored and its HOT shimmer is the standing tell. A SCRAMmed `r_b` has `Q_src` 0 and `T_mach` = `T_room` (LSG D.8), which is why V12 is not `BLOCKED` `STILL_HOT` (DA-35). **After V12** the restarted reactor leaks the uncaptured part of its heat into the room (`Q_src_room`; 516 dJ per step is the Graph's D.8 worked example, `[UNV: Graph]`) against a hull loss of `K_HULL` x 21 x (`T` - `T_AMB_MC`) / 100000 = 378 dJ per step at 42000 mC: the room reaches 45000 mC after about 2,770 steps (4.6 minutes, `tau_steps` 22,857) and settles near 68,300 mC (D.18). From the fifth minute lingering is damaging: at that equilibrium `e` is 23,286, `gain` is 40, `runs_critical` is 100 (10.0 s) and `runs_failed` 300 (30.0 s) by D.10 with Suit's heat gain; the 8-tile crossing is 192 ticks (3.2 s) walking and 384 ticks (6.4 s) carrying. The V12 channel completes before the room warms; cutting `scr_b` after V12 is the lingering act, and the player is told by the shimmer, never by a marker. **The tell must escalate before the damage (P2; CD-GDD-ALIGN concern, applied).** A standing shimmer from `T0` 42000 that looks the same when the room crosses `HEAT_DMG_ONSET_MC` would be a tell that never changes, so the presentation owners are asked for one visible *step* in the room's heat presentation (a band change in shimmer amplitude or rate, never a hue step into flesh or violet) at `HEAT_TELL_STEP_MC` (Tuning Knobs; `[PLACEHOLDER: F&T / Lighting]`, 44,700 mC as authored), which the D.18 projection reaches **2,477 steps (4.1 minutes) after V12 and 293 Graph steps (29 s, 1,758 ticks) before `t_onset_steps` 2,770**, never fewer than `W_MIN_WARNING_S` x 10 Graph steps per second = 100 steps (the 44,897 mC ceiling; AC-DA-133). F&T's shimmer today is a sev-2 stepped strip at the fitting (FT:942, FT:1087) with no onset step, which is why the ask exists (routed ask ASK-F&T-H1, Cross-References, OQ-DA-37). If the step is not provided, the AC stays BLOCKED and `b_reactor` is not releasable as a damaging room. `HEAT_ROOM_UNDECLARED` (ERROR, CI) enforces the declaration; `B_REACTOR_T_MC` and `HEAT_TELL_STEP_MC` (Tuning Knobs) are the levers.
- *Vacuum traverse.* *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix: conformed to Suit's registered budget, G1/G6; the one-way `RESERVE_S / 2` bound that Suit D.11 withdrew is withdrawn here too)* Suit's bound (SE D.11, AC-SE-75a, build assertion A15; registry `VACUUM_BUDGET_S`; `RESERVE_S` 90 s and `RESERVE_WARN_S` 30 s `[PLACEHOLDER: Suit]`) charges the sortie's whole unbreathable walking, out and back, against the reserve left after two pod legs: `traverse_runs + 2 x leg_runs <= (RESERVE_S - RESERVE_WARN_S) x RUNS_PER_S`, where `leg_runs` is 163 and `VACUUM_BUDGET_RUNS` is **274** (D.7). In tiles that is 22 each way when walking out and carrying a module back (22 x (4 + 8) = 264; 23 gives 276 and fails), 34 in total when carried both ways, and 68 in total walking. Authoring reads the **legs** (walk out empty, carry back), so a module can be carried out on the same reserve. B's `b_breach` is about 11 tiles each way (Manhattan, 6 x 5 room) = 132 runs, and when `b_pump` is itself decompressed its crossing adds 9 tiles each way: a worst case of 20 tiles each way = 240 runs against 274, **34 runs (204 ticks) of slack**; read as carried both ways (40 tiles = 320 runs) it would fail, which is why the lint reads legs and why the carry-both-ways figure (Boarding & Salvage D.4) is a conservative report-only line. `TRAVERSE_TOO_LONG` (ERROR, CI) fires when `traverse_runs` exceeds 274 and prints the legs, the budget and the slack; this closes AC-SE-75a's "NOT ASSESSED" once the lint runs.
- *B's open breach.* `b_breach` is a vacuum compartment bounded by `seg_b_breach` at `hp` 0 (a DESTROYED Graph state: the module's own destroyed state, since "breached geometry is not the breach state", art bible 6.4); it is geometry authored as its original type with props displaced (on the 24 px lattice, no off-grid record, OQ-V8), at most 30 % breached kit pieces and 1 to 2 web entries from the hull side if web intrusion is in MVP scope (art-director OQ-V5). The hole is drawn by the Graph's hull-segment row at size `l`, never by a static through-hole tile (`HOLE_WITHOUT_OPENING`, VA-12). Coupling (Checkpoint rulings Q9): that row is a descriptor of the Graph subject, so if Failure & Trail answers that a DESTROYED subject emits none (OQ-DA-11), the open hole itself cannot be drawn (a P1 lie by omission), not only the dried-trail count thins; fallback order: `seg_b_breach` at `hp` 1500 (FAILED, BREACH mode, patchable with zero parts, B's traverse kept because the room is authored at vacuum), then a dead non-leaky power fault on a branch V12 never serves. The room is entered only through `door_b_pump_breach`, which the player opens; the draught across the door is the tell.

**DA-29 Escape routes, fairness and death.** Every anchor that can carry an endogenously failing descriptor has a closable door within `ESCAPE_ROUTE_BUDGET_PX` of route length (`ESCAPE_ROUTE_OVER_BUDGET`, ERROR). The budget is Layout's LY D.19 (not a registry entry): `W_MIN_WARNING_S` 10 x `TICK_RATE` 60 x `V_RUN` 3 = **1,800 px**, which is 1,200 px at `V_WALK` 2 and 600 px carrying at `V_CARRY` 1 (Formula D.5). Layout supplies the read `route_length_px`; the lint is Authoring's. Estimates (Layout's read governs): `p_cryo_rad` to `door_cryo_s` about 336 px, `pf_feed` to `door_engine_n` about 480 px, `seg_b_pump_n` to `door_b_spine_pump` about 384 px, the longest at 27 % of budget. These figures are design values: every anchor, fixture, hardpoint, plate position and route length lives in the template files, and the acceptance criteria check "within +-48 px of the template value", not an "about" figure *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*. `ESCAPE_ROUTE_CARRY` covers any room where a carryable module node (a module in hand moves at `V_CARRY` 1, PC-8) shares a room with a live endogenous fault: it is an ERROR when the carry slack, 600 ticks less the carried route in px less `door_close_ticks` (taken as 60, `[UNV: Interaction]`), is below 0, and a WARN when the slack is under 120 ticks (2 s); at `pf_feed` (about 480 px) the slack is 60 ticks, a WARN that passes; a stowed HEAVY part, the 30 kg plate included, does not change `V_CARRY`, but while it is stowed run is disabled (user ruling D18, 2026-10-27), so a plate-bearing player moves at `V_WALK` 2 and the lint also prints the escape budget at `V_WALK` (1,200 px): `pf_feed` 480 px is 240 ticks with slack 300, `p_cryo_rad` 336 px is 168 ticks with slack 372, `seg_b_pump_n` 384 px is 192 ticks with slack 348 (each = 600 - walk ticks - `door_close_ticks` 60), the longest being 40 % of 1,200 *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*. **Scope against the decision carry** *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix, Boarding & Salvage ASK-BS-D4 (e))*: `ESCAPE_ROUTE_BUDGET_PX` (600 px carrying) binds the route from an endogenously failing anchor to a closable door, and `ESCAPE_ROUTE_CARRY` applies only to a room where a module node shares the room with a live fault; it does **not** bind the 1,008 to 1,344 px decision carry of DA-48 (a chosen act, not an escape from a gated failure), so no exemption is needed and neither lint reads that route; a lint that rejects the ruled route fails AC-BS-52 (AC-DA-161). `CORRIDOR_HEAD_NARROW` (144 px head clearance at any anchor that can reach severity 4) is Layout's id, cited. **No authored instakill without a tell:** B's breach is entered by the player's own act through a door whose draught is the tell; A's lethal cold room is reached past a frost line the beam shows; every live fault has its endogenous 10 s fairness window (`W_MIN_WARNING_S`, LS-13, endogenous transitions only; exogenous causes are not gated, so each authored exogenous hazard needs a visible precursor and a survivable retreat). Death is lethal including friendly fire (user decision) and MVP death is restart from the last autosave, which Save/Load owns (SL; cited, not restated); **no floor plan contains a one-way point**: `RETURN_PATH_TRAVERSE` (ERROR) requires a door path from every room back to `entry_room_key`, with any vacuum or cold stretch inside the traverse bound. A sane player can always back away; a carrier (`V_CARRY`) only after dropping cargo (Horror Entities C-HE-8). No room with a single exit has a floor span under 144 px (`DEAD_END_NARROW`, ERROR, Horror Entities E-HE-26; the `pod` stub is exempt, its section always dormant); every MVP room is at least 3 tiles on its short axis, so the lint passes as authored.

**DA-30 Story slots: reserved, empty, three roles.** Every template carries `story_slots: []` (Layout's `STORY_SLOT_LIST` ERROR if absent; `MAX_STORY_SLOTS_PER_SECTION` is 0 in MVP, LY:1040). The three Vertical Slice roles CAUSE, VERB and ABSENCE and the eye-line rule (trail, then source, then hatch; art bible 6.5) are documented as that slice's grammar, and **zero slots are authored**. What *is* MVP content, because it is a Graph fact or a Layout door state and not a narrative placement, is: dried trails, door leaf states (closed and open only, DA-8) and the cryo-bay pod states (closed-frosted, open-dark, smashed). Who was in the other pods is the narrative-director's; the default is one open-dark pod (the player's) and the rest closed-frosted with no silhouette (OQ-DA-8). In MVP, no text, no names, no bodies; empty suits in three poses are Vertical Slice. This is a scope limit, not a schema closure: the slot list and its three roles are what procgen inherits, and diegetic lore fragments (Alpha; found and assembled, never a linear authored story) are the narrative-director's to define and would fill `story_slots` only in templates released to the stage that owns them. `STORY_SLOT_FILLED_IN_MVP` (ERROR, CI) accepts no waiver.

**DA-31 Containment colours and the PD-1 exceptions.** Lighting's containment lint (LO-33, PD-1) colours each body's compartment adjacency so that no two compartments within `R_DRAW_MAX_PX` 126 + `WALL_FOOTPRINT_DEPTH` 24 = 150 px share a colour (126 px is Lighting's published value, the FIXTURE `r_draw` at 1000 permille now that the pool is drawn to `r_off`; the earlier 122 was stale, G1) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*, and its C3 lint (rewritten 2026-10-27, LO-33) defines spill per wall face, s = max(0, r_draw - d_face - WALL_FOOTPRINT_DEPTH), on faces backing onto another compartment (hull walls exempt), and passes a placement whose origin is at least r_draw - 24 = 102 px from each such face. Compartment sizes follow the art bible's density table and Layout, never the C3 lint (art bible 6.4 is locked; C3 is a spike input). A non-shadow FIXTURE sits at least 102 px (at full output) from any interior face that backs onto another compartment where the room allows it; elsewhere it is still placed (`fixtures_min` 1) and logged as a PD-1 exception with its spill depth. A room cannot pass with any placement only when two opposing interior faces are under 2 x 102 = 204 px apart; every other MVP room has at most one interior face per axis. Recount (Authoring's estimate; the lint's output governs): **2 of 14 are PD-1 exceptions as built**, `spine_corridor` (144 px between its north and south interior faces, s 30 px at the centre line at full output) and `b_pump` (192 px between its spine and breach faces, s 6 px at the centre line); `a_spine` and `b_spine` (144 px wide) pass only with each fixture in a stretch where one side is hull (`a_spine` clear of the `a_dock` run y [216,456); `b_spine` beside the exterior notch, y [432,552), or along `b_pump`'s run where its west side is hull), which is a placement constraint, not an exception. Whether `b_dock`'s west wall counts as backing onto another compartment after the weld (it faces the home `airlock`'s east wall across two wall strips, not lit floor) is `[UNV: Lighting]`; if it does, `b_dock` (192 px) becomes a third, post-weld-only exception. The greedy colouring of each legal body (home, A, B, and home + B after the weld) needs **3 colours**, within `CONTAIN_COLOURS` 4 `[PLACEHOLDER: Lighting]` (Formula D.8); the report is an output, run with every door closed and with every door open (the proximity edge is geometric, so both give 3), never a re-shaping instruction. If the number ever exceeds `CONTAIN_COLOURS`, that is a CONFLICT paragraph to Lighting, not a silent edit of a room. **Lighting ruling D31 (2026-10-27):** the residual bounds of the containment mechanism are accepted as scoped MVP exceptions until Vertical Slice B (static authored spill at most 24 px, unlent glowstick spill at most 60 px beyond a one-cell wall, slot-swap fade at most 36 frames, PD-1b prop-lee leak at most 2 registered subjects in view; owner technical-artist, closing trigger spike step 4 or VS-B). The two logged exceptions are measured against that bound: `b_pump` at s 6 px (126 - 96 - 24) passes; `spine_corridor` at full output would be s = 126 - 72 - 24 = **30 px**, 6 px over, and position cannot close it (144 px between faces fixes `d_face` 72 at the centre line) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*. **Resolved 2026-10-27 — delegated ruling R7 (re-review; user delegated to best judgment): the bound stays the user's 24 px and `spine_corridor` is brought within it by the least invasive change, a placement output cap.** The corridor's two fixtures `fx_corr_1` and `fx_corr_2` are authored with `base_pm` 750 (their served maximum; Lighting names `base_pm` as an Authoring lever, LO-33 (i)), so `r_off` = 120 px on the integer chain (R 144, `LIT_OFF_PM` 120; 754 permille is the ceiling, 755 gives 121) and **s = 120 - 72 - 24 = 24 px**, at the bound. The cost is small and stated: the corridor pools' `r_lit` falls from 122 to 115 px (`LIT_ON_PM` 150), the corridor reads a little dimmer than the rooms off it, and no room is resized, no fixture moved and no dressing added. `PD1_EXCEPTION_LOG` therefore lists `spine_corridor` at 24 px and `b_pump` at 6 px, both within the bound, and OQ-DA-48 is closed *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review, delegated ruling R7)*.

**DA-32 Loaded sets and subject budgets.** (registry: `legal_loaded_sets`) World-total lints run per *reachable loaded set*, not over the triple (Layout D.16's wording is "over the loaded set"; the Interaction subject cap and the Emitter caps are per loaded set). The sets follow the weld graph: a set is the home body plus at most one unwelded derelict being visited, where the home body is `{home}` before the weld and `{home, B}` after it. (provisional — Web Map & Transit undesigned; orchestrator ruling: R9's three sets are replaced by the systems-designer's five, because B stays loaded as part of the home body after the weld, so a post-weld sortie to A is reachable.)

| Set | Meaning | Tiles (`TILES_WORLD_MVP` 600, WARN at 540) | Fixtures (cap 28) | Lanterns (cap 20) | Interaction subjects `S_int` = F + L_seed + C (cap 120 `[PLACEHOLDER: Interaction]`) | `S_total` = S_int + 30 (cap 192 `[PLACEHOLDER: Emitter]`) |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| S0 `{home}` | the wake | 182 | 8 | 3 | 50 + 2 + 18 = 70 | 100 |
| S1 `{home, A}` | sortie to A | 300 | 14 | 5 | 78 + 6 + 18 = 102 | 132 |
| S2 `{home, B}` | sortie to B (unwelded) | 331 | 15 | 6 | 90 + 5 + 18 = 113 | 143 |
| S3 `{home+B}` | welded, no sortie | 331 | 15 | 6 | 90 + 5 + 18 = 113 | 143 |
| **S4 `{home+B, A}`** | post-weld sortie to A | 449 | 21 | 8 | 118 + 9 + 18 = **145** | 175 |

`F` is the declared interactables (hardpoints, two face subjects per door, valves, hull segments, sockets): home 50 (48 + the two scrubber slots of DA-47), A 28, B 40; `C` is the cap-stub worst case (at most 6 modules x 3 ports = 18); the constant 30 is `PLAYER_DROP_RESERVE` 24 + actors 4 + horrors 2 (`HORROR_SUBJECTS_MAX` `[PLACEHOLDER: Horror]`; registry `subject_budget`). As a loaded-set sum S4 reaches 145 (the design review's 143 plus the two scrubber slots of DA-47), 25 over `INTERACT_FIXTURE_BUDGET` 120, while staying inside the Emitter's 175 of 192, and S2/S3 are 113 with 7 subjects of slack *(Amended 2026-10-28 — delegated ruling G2: B carries three loose placements, so home 2, A 4, B 3)*. **User ruling D37 (2026-10-27): option (1) is adopted, Interaction registers interactable subjects by body adjacency or proximity and not by "loaded"**, so the loaded-set sum above is the conservative upper bound the lint prints and the figure the budget is judged on is the registered neighbourhood (D.17, ruling paragraph) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*. The options below are kept as the record of what was weighed before the ruling. Options, in order (Checkpoint rulings Q4): (1) **ASK-I1 (primary)**: ask Interaction to register interactable subjects by body adjacency or proximity and not by "loaded", so a welded-in far-away section does not spend the budget (fixes the whole class); (2) a **joint ask to Interaction and the Emitter**: `INTERACT_FIXTURE_BUDGET` at least 144 *together with* `MAX_EXPOSURE_SUBJECTS` at least 224 (Emitter-owned, registry range 128 to 384), because Interaction's I-7 requires `INTERACT_FIXTURE_BUDGET` + `PLAYER_DROP_RESERVE` <= 144 (75 % of `MAX_EXPOSURE_SUBJECTS` 192) and ceil((144 + 24) x 4 / 3) = 224; this is a two-owner change, not an Interaction knob turn; (3) **Authoring's own fallback, changing no other owner's number** (the Graph confirmed on 2026-10-27 that a VENT or LOAD may be authored bare, LSG:182, so OQ-DA-23 is answered): a named diet of at least 24 subjects in S4 (candidates and their readability cost are listed in Edge Cases); (4) gate the pod so A cannot be visited after the weld, rejected as a gate on sortie freedom. Lint `SUBJECT_BUDGET_SET` (ruling D37) is ERROR when a registered neighbourhood exceeds the budget and prints the loaded-set sum beside it as an INFO upper bound; it no longer WARNs on S4's loaded-set sum, and the budget value itself stays `[PLACEHOLDER: Interaction]` *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*. The runtime contract Authoring states and cannot enforce: **a dormant section must have unregistered its subjects (IA-37) or the weld breaches the subject cap**; when a registration fails the object "fails dark" and reads as a hallucination, which is a P1 violation on the content side. Authored emitters are linted per set against `MAX_EMITTERS` 96 `[PLACEHOLDER: Emitter]` and the Emitter's derived 70. **Cumulative table and intended route (design review 2026-10-27).** D.17 now carries the cumulative `S_int` / `S_total` / I-7 table with the instruments (In-World Instruments' indicative home 9, A 4 plus one battery partner, B 5) and the four console anchors that OQ-DA-38 and OQ-DA-39 ask for (POD in the three dock rooms, DOCK in the home airlock): with both adopted S4 is 166 and 196, and S2 and S3 are 128, over 120 by 8 *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)*. The intended route is ASK-I1 (adjacency registration) or a joint raise to about 166 with `MAX_EXPOSURE_SUBJECTS` about 254; the 25-subject diet is the last resort, because it removes restorable modules from a game whose pillar is that wrecks have usable organs (P5) and it cannot reach 120 with those subjects placed. S4 reachability is never gated *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*. Ruling D37 selects the intended route (ASK-I1); the diet is not needed and is kept for the record only (with DA-47's two slots counted and B's fourth loose placement removed it lands exactly at the budget, 145 - 25 = 120, with no slack, and it fails once the instruments and consoles are placed: 141 against 120) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* *(Amended 2026-10-28 — delegated ruling G2: B carries three loose placements, so home 2, A 4, B 3)*.

**DA-33 Prop density and the generator rules as content lints.** The art bible's generator rules 1 to 8 are CONTENT lints here, so a later generator can reuse them: (1) continuity, every authored pipe ends in a terminal fitting or continues (`PIPE_DEAD_END`); (2) one trail per source anchor, every trail maps to a Graph fault (`TRAIL_NO_SOURCE`, `TRAIL_COUNT_AREA`); (3) hero cap, at most one periodic-motion medium in a non-machine room (`HERO_CAP`); (4) one residual light per wreck (`RESIDUAL_LIGHT_COUNT`, DA-27); (5) seams only at joins (`SEAM_NOT_AT_JOIN`); (6) intrusion only in breached rooms, from the hull side (`INTRUSION_OUTSIDE_BREACH`); (7) the pipe band, rows 29 to 58 of the 72 px face (0.4 to 0.8), clear of props (`PIPE_BAND_BLOCKED`); (8) a clear path of at least one tile (`CLEAR_PATH_BLOCKED`, Layout's id, cited). Densities follow art bible 6.4: cryo bay wall props 1 to 2 per 10 tiles, floor at most 10 %, 1 moving medium, 1 trail; corridor at most 1 wall prop per 10, floor at most 5 %, 0 media; machine room 3 to 4 wall props per 10, floor 20 to 30 %, 3 to 6 media; a breached derelict section as its original type but displaced, 2 to 4 dried trails, at most 30 % breached pieces; grafted home-ship rooms +10 % crew clutter. Counting conventions (so the lint is unambiguous): density is evaluated at the template's *build state* (a fault that starts later is a runtime consequence, legal); a moving medium is a medium with *periodic* motion (a drip, a rotor, a bellows, a sight-glass pulse), so the heater, drawn as a steady coil-glow ramp, is not one and the cryo bay has exactly one (the drip); a trail is one *source anchor*, however many sprites (fan, crust, floor run) F&T draws from it; wall props are counted per 10 tiles of front-face wall run (which runs bear a face is the Pipeline's, OQ-V7); floor coverage is the non-walkable set-dressing footprint over the room's floor tiles. Wall-mounted fixture housings occupy face rows 59 to 72, above the pipe band (art-director proposal for the art spike). No floor prop's lit albedo exceeds Graphite lit where a trail could run (checklist 13; `FLOOR_PROP_TOO_BRIGHT`, WARN, asset-level).

**DA-34 The truth-witness rule and the falsifier map.** Every authored subject that the Perception Filter could misrepresent (a gauge, a breaker, a valve, a salvageable module, a live fault) has at least one consequence-channel witness in the same compartment: a trail (live or dried), a frost line, a lantern on or off, a lit or dark fixture on its branch, or the room's own temperature or pressure effect. The fault table's last column is the falsifier map; AC-V5 requires a witness for every fault and state. Lint `TRUTH_WITNESS_MISSING` is **WARN in MVP and becomes ERROR when the Perception Filter lands** (so the BOOT subset does not grow). One flagged gap: A's failed scrubber is a module whose failure leaves no pixel (O2 leaves no trail, FT-18), so its witnesses are the closed-list witnesses of LSG:44 (a lamp-free dead room) plus SCAN, the honest MVP instrument (Boarding & Salvage BS-17, which answers OQ-V10); undisturbed dust and Graphite-static pipes on its branch are supporting verdict grammar, not witnesses of record. The classification of structure and props as "truth" is `(provisional — Perception Filter undesigned)`. **Instrument placements (In-World Instruments IW-31).** Gauges are In-World Instruments' surfaces; Authoring authors their placement as a Kit placement record `{instrument_id, node_ref or room_key plus quantity, mount (WALL_FACE or MACHINE_PANEL), host_face}` (the record kind is a Layout schema ask, LY-4) and lints it with `INSTRUMENT_NO_BINDING` (every Graph gauge is bound and registered as an OBJECT subject for `lit(ref)`, so none silently drops out of the lie set, Sanity EC-SN-57), `PATCH_OVER_OVERRIDE` (IW E-IW-38) and the advisory `LIE_SURFACE_FLOOR` (at least 3 lie-eligible surfaces per site, so a scan-everything habit is not dominant; Sanity D.12, AC-SN-134, OQ-SN-11). The instrument rows themselves, and a second battery reading on A as the partner for `inst_b_a1_stock`, wait on the subject budget (OQ-DA-38), because every instrument is an Interaction subject counted in D.17.

**DA-35 Authored initial state is data, listed and diffed.** Every authored initial value (`hp`, `T0`, stock, charge, `breaker_closed`, `switch_on`, valve `open`, `fail_since_step`) is a single row in the template and is read back by the headless harness as a state diff against `default_state()`, so a template edit changes `state_hash` (LY-26) and nothing is hidden. Values beyond defaults:

| Section | Authored initial state |
|---|---|
| `home_ship` | `j_cryo` `breaker_closed` false; `fx_corr_1` and `fx_corr_2` `base_pm` 750 (DA-31) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review, delegated ruling R7)*; `v_o2_cryo` VALVE `open` false (the closed-valve staging; the valve-OPEN contingency of DA-16 is a one-row swap if the Graph's gated-wear text reverts) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*; `v_fuel_feed` and `v_clt_isolate` `open` true; `r_main` running with `ft_main` `stock_ml` full; `ot_main` `stock` 3,000,000 mGU; `res_main` `stock_ml` 20,000; `b_main` `stock` 2,160,000 dJ (60 % of `BATT_CELL`, a design target); `cab_trunk` `cap` 2161 W; `bcn_main` dark (beacon off, 0 W requested); `door_engine_n` OPEN, every other door CLOSED; `p_cryo_rad` 3800 with `fail_since_step` -60 (DA-15 #1); `d_cryo_exposed` 2000; `pf_feed` 3999; room `T` per C.1; the six lamps of the four non-cryo rooms `switch_on` true and served, `fx_cryo_2` `switch_on` true and unserved |
| `derelict_a` | `j_a_main` `hp` 2000, `breaker_closed` false; `j_a_res` `breaker_closed` false; `b_a1` `stock` 540,000 dJ, `b_a2` `stock` 1,620,000 dJ; `scr_a` 2000; `pump_a` 6000; `pa_frozen` 2500 `forced_mode` FREEZE, `fail_since_step` -14400; `res_a` and `rad_a` `forced_mode` FREEZE; `ot_a` `stock` and `res_a` `stock_ml` low (about 10 % of the def capacity, a design target); `door_a_spine_life` OPEN, other doors CLOSED; `T` per C.2 |
| `derelict_b` | `r_b` SCRAMmed, `T_mach` below `OVERHEAT_OFF_MC`, `ft_b` `stock_ml` 10,000 (half of the 20,000 cap) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*; `res_b` `stock_ml` 16,000; `j_b_main` is not listed (it stays at the default, CLOSED, as B's bus hub; the seam breaker is the seam sub-edge's, created OPEN by `weld_join`, DA-20) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*; `j_b_res` `breaker_closed` false; `b_b1` `stock` 432,000 dJ; `ot_b` `stock` half; `scr_b` 6000; `pb_leak` 2900, `fail_since_step` -14400; `seg_b_pump_n` 6999 `fatigued`; `seg_b_breach` 0; all doors CLOSED; `T` per C.3 |

**Fuel is non-binding in MVP (the one depleting consumable without a clock of its own)** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*. Stocks: `ft_main` 20,000 mL (full), `ft_b` 10,000 mL, none on A. Burn is `FUEL_UML_PER_W` 120 micro-mL per served W per step (LSG): the home reactor at its standing 1880 W burns 0.2256 mL per step, a runway of about 88,650 steps = **about 148 minutes**; `r_b` serving B's 1240 W burns 0.1488 mL per step, about 67,200 steps = **about 112 minutes**; after the weld with both running each reactor delivers 1560 W (DA-20), about 0.187 mL per step, so the pooled runway is longer still. Both runways exceed any MVP session, so fuel never binds by burn; the fuel clock that does bind is the FUEL-DRIP leak (fault #4, 1 mL per step at `hp` 3999 and growing as the flange wears while unpatched, LSG D.15 FUEL-DRIP), which the patch treadmill holds. AC-DA-112 asserts the stocks.

**DA-36 Count tolerance is one-sided.** LS-26's "about" counts (nodes about 40 / 30 / 35; edges about 55 / 45; LOADs about 8) are targets, not bounds. `NODE_COUNT_DRIFT` (WARN) fires only when `authored > target + max(floor(target x NODE_DRIFT_PCT / 100), NODE_DRIFT_ABS_MIN)` with `NODE_DRIFT_PCT` 20 and `NODE_DRIFT_ABS_MIN` 3; a count below target is INFO, because the Graph's cost model (LSG D.16) is monotone in count, so fewer is never a defect (Formula D.2). The hard bounds are the Graph's: total authored nodes at most 120 (LSG:787; 104 here), `MAX_GRAPH_NODES` 4096 / edges 8192 (registry, irrelevant at this scale), and the 800-node content WARN (LSG:915; irrelevant at MVP scale, said so). Also lints from the Graph's content-lint criterion: the D.12 fragility sum per compartment (`FRAGILITY_SUM`, WARN above 500,000 ppm), `delta_T_eq` per heated compartment (`HEATER_DELTA_T`, WARN above 60 C; the home cryo bay's single heater is the case checked), and any liquid pipe authored below `FREEZE_ON_MC` without `forced_mode` FREEZE (`FROZEN_ROOM_UNFORCED`, ERROR, CI: any PIPE, RESERVOIR, RADIATOR or liquid tank in a room whose `T0` is below `FREEZE_ON_MC`, DA-40).

**DA-37 Nothing authored is a marker, a lie or a combat placement.** In MVP, no label, icon, arrow, rating or text on any authored piece (VA-13; VA-13's ban targets UI-like markers, not future diegetic lore fragments, which are the narrative-director's to define and would enter through `story_slots`, never as a marker); no "phantom", "false door", "fake trail" or "decoy" record in any template (the Perception Filter corrupts the module-body channel at runtime, never the data); no entity, weapon or enemy placement (tools repel); no loot roll, rarity or tier; no meter, minimap or HUD icon for the visible unknown; no flesh (hue 5 to 30, S 20 to 60 %, V 45 to 85 %) or violet (hue 270 to 330, S above 25 %) pixel in any kit sheet or item sprite, and the lit sample of Hull Grey under every Tungsten source stays at hue 36 degrees or more (`PALETTE_SANITY_BAND`, asset-level, ERROR). A wreck is monochrome before power returns: the chromatic content of a derelict frame is closed to the player's beam, the residual lamp, thrown Chemlight pools, live fault glows and the dock status glow of DA-46 (a dim point inside a dock room only, at a luminance ceiling below the residual lamp's) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* (AC-V2 is the measurable form of "darkness is honest"). The anti-pillar guard rails of Player Fantasy are enforced by these lints and by the review checks in Acceptance Criteria.

#### G. The kit, the lint and the workflow

**DA-38 The kit manifest is data, shaped for procgen.** The manifest (registry: `kit_manifest_schema`) is a table of rows `{piece_id, category, footprint_cell, layer, flags, variant_tokens, layout_record_kind, count_bucket}`; no row names a template, so the Vertical Slice generator (VS-A) assembles from the same rows. Categories: floor, wall, door, pipe mount, fixture housing, machinery node, prop, storytelling (empty at MVP), breached variant, shared. Cells follow art bible 8.2 (floor 48 x 48; wall 48 x 96 = face 72 + cap 24; door frame plus leaf 96 x 96; machinery node 96 x 96 with a moving-medium region of at least 16 x 16; props 48 x 48, 48 x 96, 96 x 48; seam bead 4 px Hull Grey). Layers are floor, wall face, cap and prop; flags are `SOLID`, `OCCLUDES_LIGHT` and pipe-band clearance; variant tokens are `_worn`, `_breached`, `_frosted`, `_open`, `_smashed` and the door states. This GDD does not restate LY-4's field tables: it names the record kind a piece instantiates.

| Group | Piece stems (art bible 8.5 naming) | Count (maintained + `_worn`) | Cell, layer | `layout_record_kind` |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Floor | `floor_base`, `floor_plated`, `floor_threshold` (door sill) | 3 + 3 | 48 x 48, floor | Room tile / Kit placement |
| Wall | `wall_straight`, `wall_inner`, `wall_outer`, `wall_cap`, `wall_end` | 5 + 5 | 48 x 96, wall face + cap; `SOLID`, `OCCLUDES_LIGHT`; face at most 72 | Wall run |
| Wall port (exterior) | `wall_port` (round Foundry port; glass is Void Steel with one highlight) | 1 + 1 | 48 x 96; `SOLID`, **not** `OCCLUDES_LIGHT` (a Layout ask) | Kit placement on a hull run |
| Door | `door_round` frame; leaf states `closed`, `open`, `cut`, `welded` | 5 + 3 | 96 x 96 | Door (`cut`, `welded` kept for the socket seam and VS) |
| Pipe mount | `mount_bracket_straight`, `mount_bracket_elbow` | 2 + 2 | wall band 0.4 to 0.8 of the face | Anchor |
| Fixture housing | `fixture_housing`, `fixture_housing_emergency` (hanging low), `env_shared_beacon_housing` (Attention's A6 stem; a shared piece, tallied in this row at MVP) | 3 | 48 x 48 / 24 x 24; the lit state is the same sprite with the light on | Fixture anchor |
| Machinery nodes | O2, heat and coolant, power, hull-plate module | 4 | 96 x 96, medium at least 16 x 16 | Hardpoint |
| Module bodies beyond the nodes | up to 9, in four loop families sharing one silhouette grammar (O2 ring clamps, power square boxes, coolant padded collars, fuel bolted flange) | up to 9 | per Item DB `cell` | Hardpoint |
| Props | crate, locker, bunk, table, console, chair, tool rack, canister, cover plate | 9 (+ the worn set is derived) | 48 x 48, 48 x 96, 96 x 48 | Kit placement / Loose slot |
| Cryo pod wall prop | `prop_foundry_cryo_pod_closed_frosted`, `_open_dark`, `_smashed` | 3 | 48 x 96, wall | Kit placement (gap K-1) |
| Pod-dock fitting | `env_foundry_fitting_dock` | 1 | 48 x 96 on a hull run | Kit placement (gap K-2) |
| Docked podcraft placeholder | `prop_shared_podcraft_docked` | 1 | 96 x 96 prop cell (art bible 8.2 has 96 x 96 only for machinery and doors) | Kit placement (gap K-3) |
| Breached variants | torn wall straight, buckled wall corner (no through-hole) | 2 | per slice | Kit placement |
| **Dialect subtotal (Foundry)** | | **48 maintained + 14 worn = 62** | | art bible 6.3 sizes about 50 |
| Shared pipes | 4 loops x (straight, elbow, T, terminal) x 2 orientations | 32 | per 8.2 diameters | Anchor |
| Shared trails | 5 behaviours x 3 sizes (F&T's table of record; Graph descriptors, not placed by Authoring) | 15 | 48 / 96 / 144 | (Graph descriptor) |
| Shared seams | 3 shapes x (fresh, old); 4 px bead | 6 | | Kit placement |
| Web intrusion | up to 4 (B's breach only; 0 if web is out of MVP scope, OQ-V5) | up to 4 | 96 x 96 / 144 x 144 | Kit placement |
| Cap plate, plate position, cut-ragged edge | `seam_shared_cap_plate`, plate piece, `seam_shared_cut_ragged` | 3 | 48 x 48 / 48 x 96 | Layout PROJECTOR presentation pieces (LY-15; gap K-4) |
| Glowstick world sprite, generic salvage crate | `prop_shared_glowstick_inert`, crate | 2 | small | Loose slot |
| Dust mote | `vfx_dust_mote_01` (1 px) | 1 | | none (F&T / technical-artist) |
| **Shared subtotal** | | **about 63** | | art bible 6.3 sizes about 60 |

**About 125 pieces against the art bible's MVP sizing of about 110: about +14 %**, essentially all due to the `_worn` set and the extra module bodies. Mitigations in order: derive the `_worn` sheets from the maintained sheets by a tool pass (ADR candidate: worn-atlas derivation tool); share silhouettes per loop family; cut optional props to 8; accept the remainder and log it to the producer (AC-V12). **Kit gaps filed against the art bible:** K-1 the cryo-pod wall prop in three states is absent from ART 6.3 although 6.4 says "the pods are the wall" (and `smashed` is not an 8.5 state token); K-2 the pod-dock fitting; K-3 the docked-podcraft placeholder needs a 96 x 96 prop cell; K-4 the cap plate, plate position and ragged cut edge are absent from the shared sets though the seam bead is present; K-5 the exterior wall port; K-6 the EMERGENCY and beacon housings; K-7 the `worn` variant token; K-8 the MVP machinery-node note omits fuel and covers 3 of 13 module defs; K-9 stub pieces for severed pipe and cable ends after a cut (Boarding & Salvage and the Item DB own them). The inventory test (Layout AC-143) is that every manifest piece maps to a Layout record kind and every Layout kit-bearing record kind has at least one manifest piece; the pass line is zero unmapped in both directions (`KIT_PIECE_UNMAPPED`); a piece with no home in the schema is a Layout defect to file.

**DA-39 Visual rules adopted as content rules (art-director VA set).** VA-1 every authored piece is a Foundry kit piece named in the manifest, no off-manifest sprite. VA-2 `home_ship` uses the maintained atlas, wrecks `_worn`, no Layout wear field. VA-3 no violet or flesh pixel and lit-sample hue at least 36 degrees under every Tungsten source. VA-4 every live-fault anchor has a clear floor run at least one tile wide and a free approach, so the trail can be seen to worsen from across the room (`ROOM_EXCEEDS_FRAME` exceptions logged). VA-5 no loose slot, prop or fixture sits on a fault's maximum floor run. VA-6 the visible-unknown host room has an exterior port within 48 px of `exterior_anchor_px` and no competing lit light. VA-7 at wake the EMERGENCY lamp is the only lit lamp (FIXTURE, NODE_LANTERN or BEACON emitter) in the cryo bay and the beacon is dark *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*. VA-8 every dead fault is a Graph subject; no static loop-vocabulary scorch, soot, frost, stain or puddle exists in any kit tile; **exemption:** tool scorch on a cut door leaf or ragged-cut hull piece (the crew's cutter, hue-less Void Steel, never Power Amber rimmed) is a kit state, not a loop fault. VA-9 "moving medium" means periodic motion (the heater is a steady coil glow). VA-10 every wall-flush piece has face height at most 72 px and nothing overlaps face rows 29 to 58. VA-11 web pieces appear only in a room bounded by a breached segment, entering from the hull side, drawn only under direct light. VA-12 an open breach is drawn by the Graph's hull-segment state plus the plate-position piece; static torn or buckled variants never depict a through-hole and total at most 30 % of pieces. VA-13 no label, icon, arrow or text on any authored piece; no body, no skin. VA-14 a dead room's absence is undisturbed dust (floor dust unmarked). VA-15 dust motes drift only where the compartment has air, non-directional, 1 px, slow, never imitating an airflow streak. VA-16 a dead branch's pipes are Graphite with a static pattern and a live branch carries its hue and a moving pattern, the same sheet serving both. VA-17 every authored false-able system has a world-visible truth within one beam sweep. The pixel-level numbers behind these rules live in Visual/Audio Requirements.

**DA-40 The CONTENT lint table.** (registry: `content_lint_ids`, 90 rows as the table now stands (88 plus `DECISION_REACH_MISSING` and `DWELL_BUDGET_LOW` of 2026-10-28, a registry request is owed): the 74 original rows, the two design-review rows `EMPTY_WINDOW` and `COLD_WORK_BUDGET`, the six rows added since, including the DA-46, DA-47 and DA-48 rows, the two re-review rows `LANE_NODE_SALVAGEABLE` and `THAW_REACHABLE`, and the four rows of 2026-10-28, `DOCK_GLOW_ARRIVAL_DARK`, `POD_CONSOLE_MISSING`, `DOCK_CONSOLE_MISSING` and `PLACEMENT_BAD_INSTANCE`; the registry still reads 82 and a registry request is owed *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)*) Rows are data: `{lint_id, severity, run point, reads, rule}`, so Procedural Derelict Generation consumes the same table. Run points: **BOOT** (the six-rule subset of DA-41), **CI** (the offline and continuous-integration run), **ASSET** (`tools/asset-pipeline/` over sheets, not templates). Layout's own ids are cited and never redefined: `ROOM_NO_EXIT`, `ROOM_UNREACHABLE`, `CLEAR_PATH_BLOCKED`, `STORY_SLOT_LIST`, `CORRIDOR_HEAD_NARROW`, `SOUTH_BAND_OCCUPIED`, `OUTER_ROOM_HULL_RUNS`, `SEGMENT_TILE_SHARED`, `HATCH_HINGE_AUTHORED`, `DOOR_POCKET_CLEAR`, `DOOR_APPROACH_CLEAR`, `LOOSE_ON_DOOR`, `WALL_FITTING_NO_RUN`, `FLOOR_RUN_CROSSES_BOUNDARY`, `ROOM_EXCEEDS_FRAME`, `V_RANGE`, `EXPOSED_RANGE`.

| `lint_id` | Severity | Run | Reads | Rule |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `PLACEMENT_DEF_UNKNOWN` | ERROR | CI + BOOT | placements, Item DB `def_id` list | every `def_id` exists (IDB AC-90) |
| `PLACEMENT_BAD_INSTANCE` | ERROR | CI | loose placements, Item DB `fresh_instance` | fires when `fresh_instance(def_id, count, condition_bp)` returns a reason code: a non-zero `condition_bp` on a non-module, a `count` above `stack_max`, or a `condition_bp` outside 1..10000 on a module (DA-26) *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix: Item DB IDB-15, IA-28, G1/G6)* |
| `AUTHORED_NON_INTEGER` | ERROR | CI | all authored numbers | no float (DA-4) |
| `SPAWN_BLOCKED` | ERROR | CI | spawn table, rect set | each spawn passes `can_place`, breathable, off fault runs (DA-3) |
| `DOCK_GLOW_MISSING` | ERROR | CI | spawn table, dock rooms, fixtures | each room holding an `arrive_*` spawn (`airlock`, `a_dock`, `b_dock`) has exactly one dock status glow on its docked-podcraft prop; the `pod` stub has none (DA-46) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* |
| `DOCK_GLOW_COUNT` | ERROR | CI | all templates | exactly 3 dock status glows in the world; `SELF_POWERED_LAMP_COUNT` still counts the one EMERGENCY lamp (DA-23, DA-46) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* |
| `DOCK_GLOW_LIT_AT_CONSOLE` | ERROR | CI | glow, console anchors, Emitter registry values | with the bus dead, every other emitter dark and the beam off, every CONSOLE anchor in the room is lit by the glow alone: d_c <= `DOCK_STATUS_CONSOLE_MAX_PX` (24 px) and c(d_c) >= `LIT_ON_PM` + Emitter's margin (G1, Web Map D.15; DA-46) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* *(Amended 2026-10-28 — delegated ruling G1)* |
| `DOCK_GLOW_ARRIVAL_DARK` | ERROR | CI | glow origin, `arrive_*` cell centre, Emitter registry values | c(d_arr) < `LIT_OFF_PM`, i.e. d_arr >= `DOCK_STATUS_ARR_MIN_PX` for the room's arrival cell (G2, Web Map D.15); fails a layout that lights the arrival cell (Web Map AC-WM-127a; DA-46) *(Amended 2026-10-28 — delegated ruling G1)* |
| `POD_CONSOLE_MISSING` | ERROR | CI | dock rooms, `subject_anchors` | a dock room (`airlock`, `a_dock`, `b_dock`) without exactly one POD CONSOLE anchor (OQ-DA-39, DA-46) *(Amended 2026-10-28 — delegated ruling G6, cross-GDD fix)* |
| `DOCK_CONSOLE_MISSING` | ERROR | CI | `airlock`, `subject_anchors` | the home airlock without exactly one DOCK CONSOLE anchor, or a DOCK anchor in any other room (OQ-DA-39, DA-46) *(Amended 2026-10-28 — delegated ruling G6, cross-GDD fix)* |
| `DOCK_GLOW_LUMINANCE` | ERROR | CI (asset) | glow and residual-lamp `output_pm`, hue class, `wall_port` pieces | glow `output_pm` at most `DOCK_GLOW_OUTPUT_MAX_PM` of the residual lamp's, a hue class that is not the residual lamp's, and no `wall_port` in any dock room (DA-46) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* |
| `SCRUBBER_SLOT_OVERLOAD` | ERROR until the recovery case passes, then WARN | CI | home scrubber slots, D.9, the `G0_SCRUBBER_INSTALL` result | prints the install-state `rho_pm` for each slot (851, 1147, 1156) with the DEGRADED, FAILED and DESTROYED steps (376 / 875 / 1,250 at 8 bp per step; 334 / 778 / 1,112 at 9) and the per-module install ledger of DA-47; exactly 2 slots; ERROR while any install state reaches DESTROYED and `G0_SCRUBBER_INSTALL` (AC-DA-150) is absent or failing (DA-47) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* |
