- **If the weld is attempted while a lane's node is missing or its port type is wrong** (no DUCT-port node at the O2 lane, no CABLE-port node at the power lane): the Graph's `can_join` returns `PORT_INCOMPAT` and nothing is joined; DA-10 requires both lane nodes to be real and in the dock rooms so the seam edge stays far under `TRAIL_EDGE_MAX_PX` 1152.
- **If `offset_b` is not a multiple of 24 or any B room overlaps a home room**: Layout's `can_attach` reports the lattice error or `GEOM_OVERLAP` (the Graph's `can_join` is not reached); with the authored sockets the offset is (696, 384) and no rect pair overlaps (D.13).
- **If the player opens `door_b_pump_breach` while `b_pump` is pressurised and `b_breach` is vacuum**: the draught across the door is the tell; this is an exogenous act of the player's, so the fairness gate does not apply (LS-13), and the retreat is a closable door within 384 px of `seg_b_pump_n`'s anchor. The room is authored at vacuum so no precursor is hidden (DA-28).
- **If `seg_b_pump_n` breaches while the player is in `b_pump` with `door_b_spine_pump` CLOSED**: only the `V` 20 room decompresses; ANOXIC arrives about 10 s before DECOMP; the player is the only occupant, the hairline-crack-rib ladder has run for at least `W_MIN_WARNING_S` 10 s, and the door is 384 px (128 ticks running) away (D.5, D.20).
- **If a spawn cell lies within 1 tile of a fault's maximum floor run or fails `can_place`**: `SPAWN_BLOCKED` ERROR; the pod arrival rows are one tile clear of the docked-podcraft prop (DA-3).
- **If a room has no path back to `entry_room_key`** (a template edit severs the tree): `ROOM_UNREACHABLE` (Layout) or `RETURN_PATH_TRAVERSE` (Authoring's, for the vacuum or cold stretch); there is no authored one-way point, because death is lethal including friendly fire and the restart is from an autosave (DA-29).
- **If a vacuum or cold stretch's legs exceed `VACUUM_BUDGET_RUNS` 274** (more than 22 tiles each way walking out and carrying back, for example a longer `b_breach`; a hypothetical 30-tile one-way corridor is 360 runs): `TRAVERSE_TOO_LONG` ERROR (D.7). B's nested worst case (20 tiles each way, 240 runs) passes with 34 runs of slack only when read by legs *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)*.

**Hallucination interplay (P4)**

- **If the Perception Filter reports a dead branch as "fixed" or "running"**: the same compartment carries a consequence-channel witness (dark fixtures, a static Graphite pipe pattern, a lantern off, no frost change); a low-sanity player can check by looking at the lamp. `TRUTH_WITNESS_MISSING` is WARN in MVP and ERROR once the Filter exists (DA-34).
- **If the Filter fabricates a second lit point or erases the residual light in the exterior transit view**: that is a lie about a real emitter and is forbidden by contract (routed ask to the Filter and Web Map & Transit); the data side is guarded by exactly one `residual: true` fixture per wreck and zero per home (`RESIDUAL_LIGHT_COUNT`, BOOT).
- **If a low-sanity player sees the failed scrubber "pristine" or a phantom salvage object** (Vertical Slice class d): the field check is the closed-list witnesses (the verdict trail's absence or presence, the dark lamp) plus SCAN (Boarding & Salvage BS-17), with undisturbed dust as supporting verdict grammar. A phantom has no record in a template, so nothing authored can be mistaken for it (guard rail G5).
- **If a crewmate appears as a horror in a 3-tile corridor** (class c, Vertical Slice): the corridor lets two bodies pass without contact, every room is inside the fairness budget of a closable door and a return path exists; lethal friendly harm is system-gated by Sanity and Interaction, and nothing authored creates an unavoidable contact (DA-29).
- **If a hallucination redraws a door as "welded" or "cut"**: authored interior doors only ever carry the closed and open leaf states, so a welded-looking leaf is itself the lie, and the leaf state is a Layout presentation fact the player can test by trying the door (DA-8).

**Co-op later hooks**

- **If two crew breathe in the sealed cryo bay**: the sealed clocks halve (THIN at 3.5 minutes, HYPOXIC 8.2, ANOXIC 12.8); seats 2 to 4 are Vertical Slice and are not authored, and the lints assume one crew until `spawn_cryo_2..4` exist (D.19, DA-3).
- **If seats 2 to 4 are added in the Vertical Slice**: they spawn in the cryo bay's open-dark pods, `WAKE_SINGLE_LIGHT` and `SPAWN_BLOCKED` re-run for each, and `ACTORS` 4 in the subject budget already covers four bodies (D.17).
- **If a placed reward requires another player to stand in a hazard to take it**: that would be an incentive to PvP-adjacent play (anti-pillar); the review check is that every part and module can be taken by the player who holds it, with the lethal cold room's `part_cable_splice` reached by a 5.6-second crossing and out again (D.10).

**Lint, tooling and tests**

- **If a CI content lint fails and the BOOT subset passes**: the build is blocked from merging, but a release boot is never bricked beyond the six BOOT rules (DA-41).
- **If two lint runs over the same bytes print different output**: a determinism defect; CI compares the two byte for byte, and the output is ordered by `(template_id, lint_id, record_key)` with no timestamp or machine-dependent field.
- **If a waiver names `STORY_SLOT_FILLED_IN_MVP`, or any waiver has no reason**: ERROR; there is no waiver for story slots in an MVP template, and a waiver row must carry a reason and an owner.
- **If a LOCKED row of `content_lint_config` differs from the registry** (for example `DERELICT_FAULT_COMPRESSION` edited): `TUNING_SOURCE_DRIFT` ERROR naming the row, the owner and both values; no auto-fix. A PLACEHOLDER row that differs from its owner's text is the same ERROR, so a placeholder cannot silently become a fact.
- **If an unknown `def_id` is placed**: `PLACEMENT_DEF_UNKNOWN` fails the offline lint and, as a BOOT rule, refuses the template at boot with the offending record; the runtime never meets a null def (IDB AC-90).
- **If a second self-powered lamp or a second beacon appears**: `SELF_POWERED_LAMP_COUNT` and `BEACON_COUNT` refuse at boot (EM:665); the cryo `fx_cryo_lamp` is the only EMERGENCY `node_ref` 0 fixture in the world; the three dock status glows of DA-46 are the one ruled exception, a separate class counted by the CI lint `DOCK_GLOW_COUNT`, and the boot rule keeps the BOOT subset at six only if the Emitter's EM-11 amendment exempts that class (OQ-DA-46) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*.
- **If a built template has more than 8 live faults or fewer**: `FAULT_COUNT_EIGHT` ERROR by `fault_id`; dead faults and authored states do not count, woken dead faults count for glow only (D.12).
- **If `tests/` and the gdUnit4 addon do not exist** (today): the lints run as the offline tool only and the headless tests are specifications; CI wiring is the project's test-setup story (IDB OQ 28), stated as the target, never as a passing gate.
- **If the home design edge count (34) is read against LSG's "about 55 edges" and "about 8 CABLEs"**: the edge total is INFO (below target), but the 16 power edges are twice the "about 8 CABLEs": 17 power nodes need 16 edges when every island is a tree (DA-9, DA-20). Reported as INFO, not linted; the Graph's MVP-content criterion ("about 40 nodes and about 55 edges") should read as an estimate (OQ-DA-22).

## Dependencies

Status vocabulary: **designed** (a GDD file exists, read in full for the rules cited), **in-flight** (a draft file exists and is changing), **undesigned-provisional** (no file; the contract is stated here and marked `(provisional — X undesigned)`). Direction is from this GDD's point of view. Disk state is the Glob of `design/gdd/` on 2026-10-26 when this part was written; after the sibling GDDs land, every provisional row is a confirming-sweep target.

| System | Direction | Strength | Interface (names as fixed by the owner) | Status |

| Emitter State & Light-Exposure Query | Authoring depends on it | **Hard** | emitter kinds; E1 `instantiate_section_emitters`; caps 192 / 96 / 10; EM-11, EM-14, EM-16, EM-24; AC-58 | designed |

| Onboarding (Cryo Bay) | neighbour (VS) | Soft | the cryo bay as the fixture | undesigned-provisional |

**What each downstream or neighbour must repeat in its own GDD** (so the dependency is bidirectional): Boarding & Salvage repeats that the faucet is the authored node list and loose placements, the ledger is finite, and a failed module's field read is the closed-list witnesses plus SCAN (BS-17). Web Map & Transit repeats the reachable loaded sets (DA-32, including the post-weld sortie to A), that the pod is a Layout body built from the `pod` template, that a derelict left behind goes dormant when the pod leaves (never discarded), and the exterior-view rule for the residual light (`residual_lamp_predicate`). Procedural Derelict Generation repeats that it consumes the manifest and lint rows and does not fork them. Modular Ship Assembly (on disk) repeats the lane set and the weld offset (MSA-11 does). Attention (designed) repeats AT-18's one-beacon rule (it submits `set_beacon` to the one beacon the data holds and never moves or adds one) and that residual and EMERGENCY lamps are not modulated. The Perception Filter repeats that authored data is truth and that it must not fabricate or erase the residual light in the exterior view. Ship Body repeats DA-3 (the spawn table and the pod template).

Two dependencies are **circular-adjacent but not cyclic**: Layout names Authoring as the owner of the content lint while Authoring depends on Layout's schema (a one-way data edge each way: the schema flows down, the authored files flow up, and neither needs the other at runtime to start). The systems index lists three soft cycles; none touches this system.


## Tuning Knobs

Knobs are Authoring's own authored numbers and lint thresholds; every row names the formula or rule it comes from, so a balance pass changes the source and not the symptom. Defaults are the values written in the Detailed Design. **Solved** means the value is derived by the named formula and the range is the formula's legal interval, not a taste range. A `★` marks a value another GDD must cite by name; per R21 Authoring registers only its own artefacts (DA-44), so a `★` row is registered as the table or instance named there and never as a free constant. Owner-named values are in the pointer table after the main table and are not restated here. Rows marked **ask** are requests to another owner, not Authoring defaults. Cells tagged **ZERO MARGIN** sit at the floor or ceiling of their range or on the edge of a lint window, and each carries its recovery in its row *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*.

## Visual/Audio Requirements

*Consulted: art-director (Stage 3 proposal, all of it integrated below), with the advisor rulings R13, R23 and Checkpoint rulings Q2, Q3 binding where the art-director and the level-designer differed. "ART n.n" is a section of `design/art/art-bible.md`. Numbers marked **PROPOSED** are art-director values for the art spike to tune; everything else is quoted from a locked source. Pixel-level frame budgets belong to the Pipeline and Lighting GDDs and are cited, not redefined.*

**Scope.** Derelict Authoring draws nothing and plays nothing. It authors data (floor plans, placement records, a kit manifest, a lint), so every visual here is a consequence of that data, drawn by the PROJECTOR (Layout), the Pipeline, Lighting, Failure & Trail or Emitter State. This section specifies (a) what the authored data must make true on screen, (b) the kit art the data names, and (c) the visual rules the content lint enforces (DA-39, DA-40). **Audio** is a citation, not a decision (last sub-section).

### Event inventory: what the authored data makes visible

Class **T** = truth channel (a Graph consequence or a structural fact; exact under the Perception Filter, which may add content but never move the real boundary, ART 1 principle 1). Class **C** = corruptible module-body channel (the Filter may substitute class (b) false states; every C item has a falsifier in the map below, P4). Classification of structure and props is `(provisional — Perception Filter undesigned)`; re-check when it lands (Layout OQ 13).

| # | Event | Source data | Draws | Class | Requirement (numbers, constraints) |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| E1 | A section is instantiated | template, `mvp_world.json` | PROJECTOR (Layout) | T | The plan reads as ONE rect set: the drawn wall, door and prop extent equals the rect that stops the body and the beam (LY-7, LY-9). Floor at z 0, not Y-sorted; wall faces and props Y-sort by footprint south edge; caps never sort. Wall footprint `WALL_FOOTPRINT_DEPTH` 24; face at most 72 px for anything flush to a wall. |
| E2 | Cryo wake | EMERGENCY fixture, CLT-LEAK, dark heater | Lighting, F&T, module presenter | T | ART 2.2: ambient about 3 % on Graphite; `fx_cryo_lamp` Tungsten `#FFCC66` (`fixture_subkind_emergency`), dim, hanging low, shadow slot 6; the gutter is irregular dips of 0.3 to 0.5 s (18 to 30 ticks) no more often than every 10 to 20 s (600 to 1200 ticks), never periodic, never the deployable flicker. The frost run goes from the leak anchor to the far wall of the 7-tile room, inside `frost_reach(-2000 mC)` = 544 px at the F&T defaults, reachable at wake only because `p_cryo_rad` is back-dated to `fail_since_step` -60 (DA-15 #1; M1 accepts the back-date, OQ-DA-1 answered 2026-10-27) (F&T's smaller figure for a long-aged trail is a knob FLOOR, FT-11 / AC-23, never written as a reach here). The heater, the other cryo-bay fixture (`fx_cryo_2`, unserved behind the open breaker `j_cryo`) and the beacon are dark; adjoining rooms may be lit behind their closed doors (Checkpoint Q2). The pod row is strictly ordered; disorder only in which pods stand open. Nothing moves faster than the drip. |
| E3 | Power restored to a dead branch (V3 / V12) | Graph `served_pm`, fixture `switch_on` | Lighting recipe, Pipeline, Emitter | T | The lamp lights through Lighting's own warm-up recipe (a Lighting-owned cause); then the loop's hue appears along its pipe and the flow pattern (chevrons, dash-pulse, sight-glass) starts to move. A pipe on a dead branch is Graphite with a STATIC pattern (ART 4.2). No mask fade-in. Steps arrive at the Graph cadence (`GRAPH_STEP_TICKS` 6, 10 Hz), so the player can watch a wake travel down the pipe. The room floods with no new command (EM-13). |

No dried fuel stain on either wreck (not needed: the verdict grammar is served by coolant, power and hull). Every dried stop is at least 1.90:1 against Graphite lit `#5C6670` (`TRAIL_MIN_CONTRAST_X100` 190); interiors are edges and stains, never planes. Authored `-fail_since_step` at least `DRIED_AFTER_STEPS` `[PLACEHOLDER: Failure & Trail]` gives `dull_step` 3 of `DULL_STEPS` 4 and `glow_step` 0 at step 0. |

FAILED = rotor still, no medium moving, no lantern. A bolted patch plate only after a player PATCH. Module wear is `hp`-driven by the Item DB and is NOT the template `_worn` kit wear (below): a worn wall around a pristine module and a dark FAILED module in a maintained room are both legal. |

Authored templates never dead-end a pipe (`PIPE_DEAD_END`); a cut stub is a runtime consequence and legal. Stub art is Boarding & Salvage's and the Item DB's, not counted here. |

Never on a trail's floor run (VA-5). Lit only by the beam or room lamps; not emissive. A real item has NO violet and NO flesh pixel (phantom salvage betrays itself by that leak, ART 4.2). |

### Palette: what each authored thing may be

| Authored thing | Albedo | Emission or light | Forbidden |
|---|---|---|---|
| Floor tiles | Graphite mid `#2A303A` plus one step (two values, pattern contrast one step, ART 6.6) | none | any chromatic hue; Hull Grey as a plane |
| Wall faces, caps, frames, brackets | Graphite (mid, lit `#5C6670`); Void Steel `#0B1018` in crevices (one value step) | none | Hull Grey except thin elements (edge scuffs, bolt rhythm, bead) |
| Lamp housing (FIXTURE) | Graphite; lens Void Steel with ONE tight highlight | Tungsten `#FFCC66` when served | red; any loop hue; any glow baked into the sprite (the lit state is the light; the sprite is identical when off) |
| EMERGENCY and residual lamps | as FIXTURE | `#FFCC66`, dim | an ice-blue cast (would read as coolant); iridescence; any loop hue at MVP (orchestrator ruling: Tungsten on both wrecks, V-7; a loop-hue residual is Vertical Slice) |
| Beacon | Graphite, lens Graphite when off | Alarm Red sweep only | red albedo |
| Pipes, dead branch | the loop's shape in Graphite, static pattern | none | loop-hue emission |
| Pipes, live branch | as above | O2 Cyan `#3FD6E8`, Power Amber `#F2C230`, Coolant Ice `#C8E6F5`, Fuel Ember `#F06A28`; integrity Hull Grey `#8C949C` on seams and ribs only | a hue on a pipe of another loop |
| Trails | F&T per loop; dried = D.5 final stops | F&T glow rows only | a trail not mapped to a Graph fault; a dried fuel stop at MVP |
| Pod props | Graphite; frost on the glass only; occupant silhouette Void Steel / Graphite behind the frost | none | skin tone; any flesh-band pixel; a face (ART 6.5) |
| Items, parts, modules | Graphite plus the loop hue the item mends; tools are silhouettes | none (a glowstick is inert until activated) | rarity colour; violet; flesh |
| Glowstick light | n/a | Chemlight `#86B896`, steady then waning, radius 2 tiles | any other use of this hue |
| Dock status glow (DA-46) | the docked-pod prop's Graphite; the glow point on its standby panel | hue class `[PLACEHOLDER: art-director, OQ-DA-46]`, a dim point at `output_pm` at most `DOCK_GLOW_OUTPUT_MAX_PM` 250 permille of the residual lamp's `[PLACEHOLDER]` | Tungsten (it must never read as the residual lamp or the visible unknown); violet; flesh; a `wall_port` in its room *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* |
| Patch plates, seam bead, cap plate | Graphite body; Hull Grey only on bolt rhythm and a 4 px bead | none | a Hull Grey plane; hiding the bead |
| Web intrusion | no albedo; a shader band teal to green to bronze to gold, low saturation, only under direct light | none | the 270 to 330 degree arc; any unlit drawing |

**Sanity palette lint (asset level).** Reserved: violet (hue 270 to 330, S above 25 %) and flesh (hue 5 to 30, S 20 to 60 %, V 45 to 85 %). Authored content contains neither. Two checks, run by `tools/asset-pipeline/` (`PALETTE_SANITY_BAND`, run point ASSET): a static scan of every kit sheet and item sprite, and a lit sample of Hull Grey and lit Graphite under every Tungsten source whose hue must stay at 36 degrees or more (Hull Grey under `#FFCC66` computes to about hue 43, S 56 %, V 55 %, the closest sane colour to flesh, which is why it is a thin-element colour). Worked example: a 2 px `_worn` corner scuff under the EMERGENCY lamp samples at hue 36 or more; widened to a 6 px strip (a plane in practice) it fails review.

**The monochrome-wreck rule (measurable "darkness is honest").** Before any power is restored, the chromatic content of a derelict frame is a closed list: (1) the player's Tungsten beam, (2) the one residual lamp, (3) thrown Chemlight pools, (4) FAULT_GLOW of live faults (a dried stop carries no hue at MVP), (5) the dock status glow of DA-46 (one dim point on the docked-pod prop in a dock room, hue class `[PLACEHOLDER: art-director]` outside Tungsten and outside the flesh and violet bands, `output_pm` at most `DOCK_GLOW_OUTPUT_MAX_PM` 250 permille of the residual lamp's) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*. Everything else is Graphite, Void Steel or Hull Grey. A wreck with ambient colour is a wreck that was lit for free (ART 1 principle 1). Tested by AC-DA-52.

**Luminance ladder.** Highest first: beam core Tungsten about 92 = Coolant Ice about 91 > Power Amber about 80 = O2 Cyan about 79 > Hull Grey about 61 = Fuel Ember about 61 > Alarm Red about 47 = Graphite lit about 43 > Graphite mid about 20 > Void Steel about 5 (L*; Chemlight about 70). Two consequences: no floor prop's lit albedo exceeds Graphite lit where a trail could run (`FLOOR_PROP_TOO_BRIGHT`, a Hull Grey prop beside a dried stop leaves no contrast to spare); and the residual lamp (`#FFCC66`, about 5 degrees from Power Amber) is told apart from a power bar by saturation, shape (a static housing on a wall, not a bar with a travelling dash) and behaviour (a fixture never carries a dash-pulse). A wreck room passes the achromatic pass when every hero (beam, media, seam, unknown, trail) separates by value or shape with colour removed (ART 4.7).

### Donor wear: the `_worn` commitment (answers Layout OQ-25)

**Two wear axes never mix.** *Kit wear* is driven by the TEMPLATE (which atlas it names), applies to floors, wall faces, caps, door frames and leaves, pipe mounts, fixture housings and props, and never changes at runtime. *Module condition* is driven by authored `hp` and later player patches, applies to `mod_` bodies, and changes at runtime.

**Closed `_worn` vocabulary** (inside ART 6.2): scuff-to-bare at rounded corners, dust (matte Graphite mid, the floor's second value, never a third), crevice darkening (Void Steel, one value step, direction-agnostic), clogged grates. **Excluded:** scorch, soot, char, fuel or coolant stains of any kind (those are Graph trails, FT-3 and FT AC-44; a static soot decal is a false history, P1), rust or oxidation (ART 6.2), warm stains, anything that reads as skin. (orchestrator ruling: Checkpoint Q3 adopts the narrowing; R23's "scorch/soot" is withdrawn; the ART 6.2 note is routed to the art-director, DA-5.)

| Cue | Maintained (`home_ship`) | `_worn` (A, B) **PROPOSED** | Survives the 50 % zoom-out step |
|---|---|---|---|
| Corner scuff | none; Graphite lit edge | Hull Grey bare edge, 2 px wide at every rounded corner of wall, door and prop pieces | yes (2x2 minimum feature, ART 5.6) |
| Floor dust | 0 to 5 % of tile area | 30 to 50 %, clustered at walls, corners and the lee of props, Graphite-mid blobs of at least 4x4 px | yes |
| Panel seam | crisp 1 px Void Steel line | the line plus 2 px of dust fill | marginal; carried by the other two |
| Grates | open | clogged with the dust value | yes |
| Fastener rhythm | intact big Foundry rivets at wide pitch | unchanged (a Foundry bolt is never missing; missing bolts are Lattice's axis) | n/a |

The `_worn` set keeps Foundry geometry, rivet rhythm, corner radius and panel ratio, so the three-piece test (ART 6.3) still identifies the dialect; only surface history differs. **Mechanism (behaviour only):** an atlas swap by template; `env_foundry_tiles_alb.png` and `env_foundry_tiles_worn_alb.png` share slice names and one `layout.json`, so the PROJECTOR picks the atlas by `template_id` and no Layout field carries wear (LY:1099); a decal overlay was rejected because it needs a placement record Layout lacks (guard rail G1). `WEAR_TOKEN_MISMATCH` fails a wreck that names a maintained piece or `home_ship` naming a `_worn` one. -> ADR candidates: worn-atlas derivation tool; atlas selection by `template_id`.

**The X-1 gate (Layout X-1).** Art-director commitment: MVP, Foundry only. In ONE lit capture at the seam with the doubled bulkhead in view and the same beam on both sides (so ambient differences cannot answer), at least 4 of 5 blind reviewers name the maintained side (AC-DA-07). If the cues need a third reviewer prompt, widen the dust coverage band before adding a new cue type. If the art spike cannot meet it, X-1 is downgraded to Vertical Slice in Layout's text, not left unsupported.

### Composition by area (ART 6.4 applied)

Counting conventions: DA-33.

| Area | ART 6.4 numbers | Worked example | MVP application |
|---|---|---|---|
| Cryo bay (`home_ship`) | wall props 1 to 2 per 10; floor at most 10 %; media 1; trails 1; strictly ordered pod row | 7 x 6 = 42 tiles gives at most 4 tiles of floor dressing; one coolant leak source = 1 trail; the drip is the 1 medium; the heater is a steady coil, not a medium; the exposed DUCT is a static pipe until it goes live | the pods ARE the wall (they count as wall) |
| Corridors | wall props at most 1 per 10; floor at most 5 %; media 0 | a 3 x 13 corridor = 39 tiles gives at most 1 tile (5 % of 39 is 1.95); head clearance 144 px at any anchor that can reach sev 4 (`CORRIDOR_HEAD_NARROW`, Layout's id) | a non-shadow FIXTURE sits at least 98 px from each interior face backing onto another compartment (C3, LO-33); a 3-wide corridor with interior faces on both long sides cannot meet it, so its fixture is placed anyway and logged PD-1, and a corridor with one hull side places its fixtures there (DA-31) |
| Machine rooms | wall props 3 to 4 per 10; floor 20 to 30 %; media 3 to 6; trails originate here; clear cross-path | a 5 x 10 room = 50 tiles gives 10 to 15 tiles of dressing; depth at most 5 | machinery on walls, media facing the room, face at most 72 px |
| Airlock, pod-dock | not in the table: corridor-class plus ONE 96x96 prop (docked podcraft placeholder) and the dock fitting | floor at most 5 % | the socket hatch is the hero; after a weld the bead is hero #3 |
| Dead derelict rooms | original type, displaced; 2 to 4 dried trails per breached section; at most 30 % torn pieces; 1 to 2 web entries; media 0 | B's breach section: of N wall and floor pieces at most 30 % take a torn or buckled state with no through-hole | "displaced" is rotation-free placement variety on the 24 px lattice, not an off-grid record (`-> OQ-V8`) |
| Web exterior | 0 props | n/a | Web Map & Transit's; Authoring supplies `visible_unknown` only |

**Home-ship lamp rhythm (ART 2.3, 2.4).** The home-ship reads domestic: many small overhead Tungsten fixtures, soft, shadowless (the six shadow slots are the four beams, the beacon and one more). Every room carries at least one housing so it can be re-lit AND un-lit (P3 restraint). Wall-mounted housings sit ABOVE the pipe band, on face rows 59 to 72 (14 px; **PROPOSED**, inferred from the locked band 29 to 58); the free-hanging housing is the EMERGENCY lamp only, drawn on the cap layer at 24x24 px or less and at least 1 tile off the coolant floor run so it never hides the hero-#5 trail (the layer question is `-> OQ-V9`). The conserved-dark and lost-dark moods (ART 2.4) are produced by Lighting from the same authored fixtures, not by a second template.

**One sweep answers three questions (ART 6.0).** For every room one beam sweep answers: who built this (Foundry: round ports, big rivets, squat panels); what did people do to it (patches, a missing module at a pedestal); has the web come in (only B's breach). Tested by AC-DA-93 (a).

**Verdict grammar at MVP (ART 6.5, DA-30).** With story slots empty, the three sentences are carried only by Graph facts and kit states: *Cause* = a dried trail leading to the failed fitting near the door the crew used (eye line trail, source, hatch); *Verb* = a door leaf state, deferred to Vertical Slice at MVP (interior doors are closed or open only); *Absence* = undisturbed dust, an empty pod (open-dark), a vacated pedestal where a module was cut cleanly. Suits and bodies are not MVP (ART 6.5: never skin, never a body). The three sentences are distributed across rooms, never inside one cone. Recommended minimum per wreck: one dried trail leading to a hatch, one pod or pedestal absence. This is a visual recommendation; pod-state assignment is the narrative-director's (OQ-DA-8; default one open-dark, the rest closed-frosted, no silhouette, DA-30).

### Animation and motion constraints

| Thing | Allowed | Not allowed | Why |
|---|---|---|---|
| Kit pieces | none | idle animation, bobbing, pulsing | ART 6.4 "nothing decorative moves" |
| Door leaf | slide whole pixels per tick into the pocket on the hinge side | rotation, easing, sub-pixel slide | integer pixel pipeline (LY-9) |
| Machinery moving medium (live) | the loop's own pattern in a region of at least 16x16 | motion on a dead module (rotor still) | ART 8.2, 4.2 "dead = static" |
| Pipe flow pattern | chevrons, dash-pulse, sight-glass, on live branches only; static on dead | animating on a dead branch | P1 |
| Frost, dried scorch, dried stain, crust | none (F&T may step the dull ramp) | shimmer, sparkle, pulse | ART 3.3 |
| Drip (cryo bay) | fall frames at the drip cadence | faster than "a drip" in the wake | ART 2.2 |
| EMERGENCY lamp | irregular gutter, 18 to 30 tick dips, no more often than every 600 to 1200 ticks | periodic flicker; the deployable flicker | ART 2.2 |
| Residual lamp | none (steady) | the cryo gutter, an Attention gutter, a power-down fade for effect | LO named rule 2: every change has a cause |
| Beacon | red sweep, 128 ticks per revolution, housing rotates only while emitting | strobe; rotation when dark | ART 2.4 |
| Seam bead | none; fresh to old at 36,000 ticks | pulse | ART 6.1 |
| Dust motes | slow, non-directional, 1 px, inside lit volumes of breathable compartments only (VA-15) | directional streaming; any motion in vacuum | F&T row 24 AIRFLOW is directional truth and must not be imitated |
| Zoom ladder | every piece integer-scaled; detail survives the 50 % step (2x2 minimum) | sub-pixel detail | Pipeline ladder, ART 5.6 |

### Truth-channel classification and the falsifier map (P4)

| Visual | Class | Reason |
|---|---|---|
| Walls, floors, caps, door frames, seam bead, cap plate, plates; door leaf state | T | geometry (one rect set); Graph `door_state` |
| Fixture, residual, EMERGENCY, beacon and glowstick LIGHT | T | real emitters with `lit` from the Graph (EM-11, EM-13); a false lamp is an unlit decoy overlay, never a light (LO). The housing sprite is C-capable, its light is T |
| Live and dried trails, AIRFLOW, frost crust, hole stamp, FAULT_GLOW rim | T | Graph descriptors only (FT-3); a dried trail is its own falsifier |
| Pipe flow pattern (moving or static) | T | live versus dead from the Graph |
| Pod props, pedestals, web intrusion | T `(provisional)` | authored scenery; the Filter may add phantom copies, never edit these |
| Module body at its `hp`; dead gauges on a module; loose item sprite | **C** | module-body channel; each needs the falsifier below |
| Dust motes | T-neutral | atmosphere; carries no information |

| Corruptible visual (authored) | What a hallucination could say | Truth-channel companion within one beam sweep |
|---|---|---|
| A's FAILED scrubber `scr_a` (`hp` 2000), dark rotor | "running, intact" | **No consequence pixel exists** (O2 failure leaves no trail, FT-18). Witnesses: the room is lamp-free (T; lamp brightness is on LSG:44's closed list); the dead O2 pipe on its branch is Graphite-static and the dust is undisturbed (S, supporting verdict grammar, VA-14; neither is on LSG:44's closed list). The falsifier is the closed-list witnesses plus SCAN (Boarding & Salvage BS-17, which answers OQ-V10). This is the single flagged exception; `TRUTH_WITNESS_MISSING` is WARN at MVP (DA-34). |
| A's broken breaker `j_a_main` (`hp` 2000) | "closed, live" | dried radial scorch at the box (T); no emission along the dead bus (T) |
| A's pump stall (PUMP, STARVE, dead branch) | "pumping" | STARVE draws nothing (F&T row 6): the pump's lantern is OFF (Emitter, T) and the wide-chevron pattern is static (T) |
| B's SCRAM reactor `r_b` | "spinning, powered" | no node lantern (EM-14, T); the FAULT_GLOW rim (row 9, T) |
| A's frozen pipe `pa_frozen` | "thawed" | frost sheath on the lagging (F&T row 4, T) |
| B's dead coolant leak `pb_leak` | "fine" | the greyed frost fan with no drip (T) |
| Loose parts | "a data core, treasure" | no violet and no flesh in the real sprite (ART 4.4); weight via Suit's carry cue (T, IDB) |

Rule (DA-34, VA-17): for each of the eight live faults and each authored STATE, the fault table lists its falsifier row; a fault with no truth-channel companion is a content defect unless it is the flagged exception above.

### Visual rules and the lints that enforce them

VA-1 to VA-17 are adopted in DA-39. Enforcement: VA-1 `KIT_PIECE_UNMAPPED`; VA-2 `WEAR_TOKEN_MISMATCH`; VA-3 `PALETTE_SANITY_BAND`; VA-4 and VA-5 `LOOSE_ON_TRAIL_RUN`, `FLOOR_PROP_TOO_BRIGHT`, Layout's `CLEAR_PATH_BLOCKED` (review for the plan-view check, AC-DA-107); VA-6 `VISIBLE_UNKNOWN_NO_PORT`, `VISIBLE_UNKNOWN_COMPETITOR`; VA-7 `WAKE_SINGLE_LIGHT`; VA-8 `TRAIL_NO_SOURCE`; VA-9 `HERO_CAP`; VA-10 `PIPE_BAND_BLOCKED`; VA-11 `INTRUSION_OUTSIDE_BREACH`; VA-12 `HOLE_WITHOUT_OPENING`; VA-13 review (AC-DA-53); VA-14, VA-15, VA-16 review and the Pipeline's own criteria; VA-17 `TRUTH_WITNESS_MISSING`. (orchestrator ruling: `WAKE_SINGLE_LIGHT` scopes to `cryo_bay`, the wake ROOM, per Checkpoint Q2 and ART 2.2 "it is the only fixture in this state"; lit adjoining rooms behind a closed door do not break the wake, and the lint also asserts the beacon is dark at step 0. The art-director's stricter every-room reading is not adopted.)

All stems follow ART 8.5, `<cat>[_<dialect>]_<subject>[_<variant tokens>]_<map>.png`, lowercase, two-digit indices; the generic naming convention is superseded as ART 8.5 explains. The manifest is DA-38 (**PROPOSED** tallies, the Foundry dialect only at MVP). Groups, stems and cells: DA-38.

**Kit gaps filed against the art bible:** K-1 to K-9: DA-38 (nothing edited here; the amendments owed to the bible are `-> OQ-V1`, deliberate amendments and not silent overrides).

**Piece-count tally (risk).** About 125 pieces against about 110 (about +14 %), mitigation order and `/asset-spec` reconciliation: DA-38, AC-DA-55. **Not in this list (owner named):** trail sprites and glow rows (F&T), beam and pool cookies (Lighting), module-body wear states (Item DB), severed stubs (Boarding & Salvage), the exterior wreck silhouette and pod (Web Map & Transit, lit only by the pod's running light), HUD marks (none), empty suits and interrupted props (Vertical Slice).

### Art-side acceptance criteria, mapped

The art-director's twelve checks are carried in Acceptance Criteria under these ids: sanity palette AC-DA-51; monochrome wreck AC-DA-52; X-1 side identification AC-DA-07; the three ART 6.0 questions AC-DA-93 (a); fault falsifier rows AC-DA-50; wake single light AC-DA-33; visible-unknown port and competitor AC-DA-37; dried-stop contrast and step AC-DA-125; wear, hole, pipe-band and source lints AC-DA-06, AC-DA-49; exterior single lit point AC-DA-38; power-restored static-then-moving pipes and the no-mask lamp AC-DA-126; piece-count reconciliation AC-DA-55.

### Audio: what the authored objects emit (citation only)

Adaptive Audio is undesigned, so this GDD authors no audio rule, layer, ducking, fade or trigger. The authored objects emit exactly what their owners' descriptors say: node lanterns hum while their machine runs (EM-14), B's SCRAM rim ticks as it decays (F&T row 9), a leak hisses with its flow, and an authored hull segment may groan before it fails (the tell is visual; whether and how it sounds is the audio-director's). The mood the visuals set for the audio-director to match: a dead wreck is still, dusty and archaeological; a woken branch is a slow rhythmic hum; the cryo bay is hushed and the drip is the loudest thing in it. Perception Filter audio lies are the Filter's and never live in an authored file.

📌 **Asset Spec** — Visual/Audio requirements are defined. After the art bible is approved, run `/asset-spec system:derelict-authoring-handmade-kits` to produce per-asset visual descriptions, dimensions and generation prompts from this section (the K-1 to K-9 pieces and the `_worn` derivation first).

## Game Feel

This system has no input and no frame of its own, so its feel is the feel of what the authored data makes happen: how soon a player's act is answered by a lamp, a pipe or a trail, how long a tell lasts before a clock ends, how far a player can run to a door, and how heavy a carry across a dead room is. Every number below is either authored here, derived from an authored value by a formula in this GDD, or cited from the owner (never restated as ours). The playtest targets at the end are `[PLACEHOLDER: creative-director]` until the first playtest.

### Input-to-feedback chain (the content-shaped acts)

| Step | What happens | Latency | Owner |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | The player holds `interact` on a breaker (V3), valve (V5), fitting (V1) or crank (V12). The acknowledgement is immediate; the channel bar fills linearly | 0 ticks to the bar; V1 `PATCH_CHANNEL_TICKS` 180 (3 s) `[PLACEHOLDER: Interaction]`; V12 `CRANK_CHANNEL_TICKS` 240 (4 s); V3 and V5 per Interaction | Interaction, Player Controller |
| 2 | The completed act calls its Graph mutator (M7, M11, M13 and the like) in the kernel's command path | applied inside the same tick it completes (kernel P-phase), never drawn before commit | Interaction, kernel |
| 3 | The Graph hook chain (`graph.propagate.power`, `.atmo`, `.failure`) runs at divisor 6, so the new `served_pm` is visible to the Emitter and the presentation at the next Graph step | at most `GRAPH_STEP_TICKS` 6 ticks = 100 ms after step 2 | Graph |
| 4 | The Emitter marks the fixture lit; Lighting warms it over `LAMP_WARM_FRAMES` (placeholder 5 frames, `[PLACEHOLDER: Lighting]`) with the prompt edge and the visible edge agreeing from the first frame (LO-19) | 5 presentation frames, about 83 ms at 60 Hz | Lighting |
| 5 | The pipe pattern on the newly served branch starts to move; frost, drip and trail respond at their own cadences (F&T) | one Graph step per hop, so a wake travels down a pipe at 10 hops per second | Pipeline, F&T |

So the answer to "I closed the breaker" is a lit room within about 12 frames of the channel completing, and the whole branch following hop by hop inside a second or two: fast enough to read as the player's cause, slow enough to watch the effect travel (P1: the pipe agrees with the gauge). What Authoring contributes to this chain is the content that makes it legible: `fx_cryo_2` is `switch_on` true behind the breaker `j_cryo` (open at wake), so the room floods with no new command (EM-13); the branch is short (the cryo branch cable `cab_cryo` is the first thing the breaker feeds); and the frost trail, the dark heater and the dim lamp are all in one frame of the wake room.

### Content timings the feel rests on (all derived, with the source)

| Quantity | Value | Derivation |
|---|---|---|
| Cryo wake: lamp gutter | 18 to 30 tick dips, no more often than every 600 to 1200 ticks | ART 2.2 as authored in E2 (Lighting's `gutter_recipe` owns the generator) |
| Warm-up of the cryo bay after V3 | the room warms at about 4 mC per Graph step gross (500 W into C = 42 x `C_TILE` 3000 = 126,000 dJ/°C, LSG D.7), about 3 mC net of hull loss at 13 exposed tiles, so about 6.5 minutes (about 3,900 Graph steps, about 23,400 ticks) from -2000 mC to COMFORT at 10000 mC; LSG:839's 2.5 minutes is its 20-tile example. The slower climb is the price of the verbatim Layout fixture (V 42, 13 exposed tiles); a second heater is the PWR-OVL trigger (DA-15 #3), so `CRYO_HEATER_COUNT` stays 1 | DA-45, D.18 warm-up line, LSG D.7 |
| Every endogenous failure's tell | 600 to 1,800 ticks (10 to 30 s), never under `W_MIN_WARNING_S` 10 s = 600 ticks | P2; the Graph's fairness gate on endogenous transitions (DA-29) |
| Longest escape route to a closable door | 1,800 px = `W_MIN_WARNING_S` x `TICK_RATE` x `V_RUN` (10 x 60 x 3): 10 s at a run, 15 s at `V_WALK` 2 | `ESCAPE_ROUTE_BUDGET_PX` (Layout D.19); authored longest is `pf_feed`, about 480 px, 160 ticks at a run (D.5) |
| One-way crossing of `a_machine` carrying a module | 7 tiles = 336 px at `V_CARRY` 1 px/tick = 336 ticks = 5.6 s | D.10: critical after 364 runs (36.4 s) at `T0`, so a crossing leaves a margin of about 31 s |
| Longest vacuum or cold round trip carrying a module | 44 tiles = 2,112 px walking out and carrying back (22 each way, 264 runs = 26.4 s; the lint bound `VACUUM_BUDGET_RUNS` 274 allows no 23rd tile), 34 tiles carried both ways; B's nested worst case 20 tiles each way = 240 runs = 24.0 s, 34 runs (204 ticks) of slack *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)* | D.7 (`TRAVERSE_TOO_LONG`) |
| Reference carry (`htr_a` cut point to the pod in `a_dock`) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* | ruled 21 to 28 tiles = 1,008 to 1,344 px = 16.8 to 22.4 s at `V_CARRY` 1; provisional 28 tiles (1,344 px, 22.4 s: n = 12, d = 24) `[PLACEHOLDER — derive at spike]` *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)*; today's plan is 13 tiles (624 ticks, 10.4 s) | DA-48 |
| Fuel Ember clock from wake | FAILED no earlier than 10 minutes (6,000 Graph steps' worth: 36,000 ticks), SCRAM recoverable | DA-16, amended R5 |
| Pump-stall tell on A (full bus, `b_a2`) | 1,012 Graph steps = 6,072 ticks = 101 s of served pump before the stall (the 900-step target met with 112 steps to spare) | D.16 |
| A's lethal room out of damaging cold after waking the branch | about 12.9 minutes on the full bus (447 steps to the onset, then 12.0 minutes of return after the stall) | D.22 |
| Seam breaker closed onto a dead B | lamps dim at once, `cab_trunk` DEGRADED at step 334 (33 s), FAILED at 778 (78 s), DESTROYED at 1,112 (111 s); re-open before 778 and the trunk survives | DA-20, D.9 |
| Heat tell on B (`b_reactor`, after V12) | presentation step at `HEAT_TELL_STEP_MC` 44,700 mC 2,477 Graph steps (4.1 minutes) after V12, 293 steps (29.3 s, 1,758 ticks) before damage starts at 2,770; never under 100 steps (600 ticks) | D.18 tell lead, DA-28, AC-DA-133 `(provisional)` |
| Hull-fatigue tell on B | `seg_b_pump_n` crosses `BREACH_HP` after 30 periods (3,000 steps = 5 minutes of B-loaded time); hairline to crack to bent rib is visible for at least 10 s before the breach (P2) | D.20, D.15 |
| Patch hold before decay | `PATCH_DECAY_STEPS` 2400 = 4 minutes; a patched home leak is back at `LEAK_HP` in 4,500 steps (7.5 minutes), a derelict one in 3,500; the felt cost of this treadmill is target X-DA-9 (below), whose levers are `PATCH_DECAY_STEPS` (Graph-owned) and the home leak count | D.15, DA-25 |
| Restart race on B | the patch on `pb_leak` lands within `W` 90 s of V12; the stock crosses the floor at 123.8 s (step 1,238) and the tank is empty at 211 s; V12 channel 4 s *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* | D.21 |
| Residual lamps | A 27,000 steps = 162,000 ticks = 45 minutes loaded; B 21,600 steps = 36 minutes loaded | D.6 |
| Beacon revolution | `BEACON_SWEEP_TICKS` 128 `[PLACEHOLDER: Emitter]` = 2.13 s | Emitter registry |
| Door leaf | slides whole pixels per tick; it travels in the same tick the Graph's `area` changes (0 ticks of Layout latency) | LY-9, Layout latency row |
| Weld seam: fresh to old | `SEAM_OLD_AFTER_TICKS` 36,000 (10 minutes) | ART 6.1 |

### Weight and snap

- **Whole-pixel everything.** Leaves, plates, dust and trails move or sit on whole pixels at the integer zoom rungs; a placed prop sits on the 24 px lattice (LY-3), so a player never sees a prop one pixel off a wall. Snap target: zero sub-pixel positions in any authored record (`AUTHORED_NON_INTEGER`, `WELD_OFFSET` lattice check D.13).
- **The carry is the weight.** A module is carried at `V_CARRY` 1 (a third of a run, half a walk). The authored salvage is placed so the round trip through an unbreathable stretch to the farthest salvage and back fits the vacuum budget of D.7 (walking out and carrying back, at most 22 tiles each way, 26.4 s; B's nested worst case 24.0 s) *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)* and the farthest hazard crossing at most 7 tiles of lethal cold (5.6 s): heavy enough to feel like a burden and a decision about the route, short enough to be fair. No authored carry route passes within 1 tile of a fault's maximum floor run.
- **Nothing decorative moves.** The only moving things in a dead wreck are the player's beam and its dust; the only sound-shaped event is a lamp, a drip or a hum that the Graph is actually producing. That stillness is the weight of a wreck.
- **No hit-stop, shake or flash is authored.** Failure is felt through the tell (a longer-lasting visible trail and a changing pipe), not through an effect on the camera.

### The three authored beats (anchor moments as feel)

1. **The cryo wake (first minute).** Slowest, quietest frame in the game: ambient about 3 %, one gutter dip every 10 to 20 s, a drip as the fastest motion, a frost line the eye follows to a fitting. The player closes `j_cryo`, the room floods warm in about 12 frames, the heater begins its 6.5-minute climb (about 4 mC per Graph step gross, D.18; the price of the verbatim Layout fixture), and the player patches the fitting in 3 s of channel. Beside the door the shut valve `v_o2_cryo` waits in the same sweep: a player who lingers meets the thinning air at 7.0 minutes with the valve in view, and the first valve they turn is a lever with a price (V5, DA-45) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*. The door opens onto a lit corridor with the beacon dark; `door_engine_n` stands open and the ember glow of `pf_feed` is the next thing in view. No text, no marker (DA-45). "Warmth is spent, not owned": the flood of Tungsten is an event the player caused and paid for in power.
2. **The held-breath boarding (derelict A, first sortie).** From the pod, A is black but for one Tungsten point (the residual lamp). Arrival room: beam only, dust in the cone, a dried coolant fan at a lagged elbow. The eye follows trail, source, hatch (ART 6.5 eye line). One sweep tells the player who built it, what was done to it, and that the web has not come in. The decision beat is the dead branch: swapping the spare `mod_breaker_junction` wakes the bus, lights the pump's lantern (the FAILED scrubber's stays dark, DA-24), starts the pump-stall clock (about 100 s of tell, D.16) and doubles the speed of every wear row (`DERELICT_FAULT_COMPRESSION` 500). The cost lands as the player's own consequence, not an ambush.
3. **The weld seam (derelict B, then home).** B is hot and loud in the dark: a SCRAM rim glow, a draught from a vacuum room, a fatigued segment with a visible hairline-to-crack tell, a restart race with a 90 s window. After the weld the player stands at the doubled bulkhead and can say which side was the original ship without a prompt (Layout X-1, AC-DA-07).

### Playtest-checkable targets

The creative-director ruling (CD-GDD-ALIGN) endorses X-DA-1 to X-DA-7 as written and adds X-DA-8 and X-DA-9; the design review adds X-DA-10. The targets themselves are fixed; only the numeric thresholds are `[PLACEHOLDER: creative-director]` (tagged inline). Each round is **five testers, at least three of whom are outside the project**, matching the concept's kill/continue gate ("at least three people outside the project", game-concept MVP Definition). Five testers and 4-of-5 thresholds are a smoke signal, not a statistic: a second round of 8 or more is required before any threshold below is promoted from placeholder `[PLACEHOLDER: creative-director]`. Protocols and scoring sheets are in Acceptance Criteria (AC-DA-87 to AC-DA-93, AC-DA-135 to AC-DA-137), AC-DA-151). **The re-review adds X-DA-11** (the first scrubber install, DA-47). **Which beats a 30-minute session can test** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*: the concept's kill/continue session is about 30 minutes, which holds the home wake (X-DA-4, X-DA-7), the first sortie to A with its read, decision and carry home (X-DA-1, X-DA-2, X-DA-3, X-DA-6, X-DA-8) and the first install (X-DA-11 when `scr_a` is carried); the weld and the first B sortie (X-DA-5, X-DA-10) fall at about 45 to 55 minutes and the treadmill (X-DA-9) needs 45, so they are run in a separate session that starts from a save past the first sortie, and the 30-minute gate is never scored on them. **Reference carry per target:** X-DA-1 to X-DA-3 need none; X-DA-6 and X-DA-8 log whichever module the tester carried (the leave-it-dark outcome cuts `scr_a` by beam; the reference first sortie of DA-48 cuts `htr_a`, and the plan of record is `htr_a` first and `scr_a` as the second carry, DA-47); X-DA-10 carries nothing out; X-DA-11 carries `scr_a`.

| Id | Target |
|---|---|
| X-DA-1 | At least 4 of 5 `[PLACEHOLDER: creative-director]` first-time testers name the failed loop of derelict A within 60 s of boarding, from the dried trails alone, and point at the trail that told them *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* |
| X-DA-2 | At least 4 of 5 `[PLACEHOLDER: creative-director]` point at the residual light during transit and find its room aboard within 3 minutes without a marker |
| X-DA-3 | At least 3 of 5 `[PLACEHOLDER: creative-director]` pause (coded with gaze or utterance, AC-DA-89) or voice a cost before closing A's breaker or swapping its junction *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* |
| X-DA-4 | No tester is stationary and lost for more than 30 s in the cryo bay; at least 4 of 5 `[PLACEHOLDER: creative-director]` follow the frost trail to its fitting unprompted |
| X-DA-5 | Layout X-1 as written (seam-side identification), cited not restated; AC-DA-07 and AC-DA-91 |
| X-DA-6 | At least 3 of 5 `[PLACEHOLDER: creative-director]` testers, given the neutral prompt "tell me about what you took and what you left", answer about choice or risk, not an inventory display *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* |
| X-DA-7 | The door state (open or closed) is identified correctly from three tiles in greyscale by at least 4 of 5 `[PLACEHOLDER: creative-director]` reviewers |
| X-DA-8 | Restraint is a real strategy (P3): across 5 testers both the wake-the-branch and the leave-it-dark outcome on A occur at least once, and every tester who left it dark returned home having taken the cut by beam, and can say why they carried it or left it; the observer also logs, for every tester, the reason given for waking the branch or leaving it dark (the AC-DA-135 coding), so that a branch nobody wakes for the D.22 window shows as a false choice *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* |
| X-DA-9 | The patch treadmill is not a chore (P5 against the MVP core hypothesis): fewer than 2 of 5 `[PLACEHOLDER: creative-director]` testers describe re-patching the home leaks as tedious or a chore in the first 45 minutes, and the observer-timed share of active maintenance (channels plus walking to the leaks) in minutes 10 to 45 is at most 8 % with the plate carried and 16 % without it (D.15 computes 4.9 to 6.8 % and 12.8 to 14.7 %) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*. Levers if it fails: `PATCH_DECAY_STEPS` (Graph-owned, a routed ask) and the home leak count (3); the ledger is not changed before the playtest (DA-25) |
| X-DA-10 | A first-time tester's first sortie to derelict B (a restart race of about 2.1 minutes after V12, the floor crossing at step 1,238 (D.21) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review: was "a 3.5-minute restart race", a constant-leak figure; this pass bar, not a derived constant, sets the race length, OQ-DA-50)*, a hull breach at 5.0 minutes and decompression at 6.7 minutes of B-loaded time, reachable before any plate is carried): at least 4 of 5 `[PLACEHOLDER: creative-director]` testers restart `r_b` (V12) and finish the sortie without a SCRAM-recovery prompt, at most 1 of 5 close the seam breaker onto a dead B, and the SCRAM-recovery sub-case of AC-DA-59 is observed *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* |
| X-DA-11 | The first scrubber install at home is a taught order, not a silent run-ender (DA-47): of 5 first-time testers who carry `scr_a` home and install it with the cryo branch closed, at most 1 `[PLACEHOLDER: creative-director]` loses `cab_trunk` (DESTROYED), and at least 4 of 5 who see the tell shed the cryo branch or remove the scrubber before FAILED (step 875; 778 with the beacon lit); if more than 1 of 5 lose it, the install interlock of DA-47 option (c) is built (OQ-DA-47) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* |

## UI Requirements

**Derelict Authoring has no UI of its own; every surface that shows an authored object is owned by another GDD.** It authors files, not a screen: no HUD element, instrument, menu, prompt, marker, map overlay or notification is named or implied here, and none may be inferred from a field in a template. This is a design decision (P1, P3, the anti-pillars and DA-37), not an omission: the world is the display, so a wreck's richness, danger and history are shown by a lit room, a dried trail, a pipe that agrees with its gauge and one warm point in the dark, never by a rating, a "salvage value", an objective marker, a minimap pin, a "cleared" tick, an icon on the visible unknown or a danger meter from a fault rate.

What touches a player-facing surface, and who owns it:

| Authored fact | Shown by | Owner |
|---|---|---|
| Lamps, residual light, beacon, glowsticks, lanterns | the world, as light | Emitter State, Lighting |
| Faults, dried trails, frost, hole, airflow | the world, as trails and glow rows | Failure & Trail |
| Module `hp` and condition, loose items | the sprite and the interact prompt | Item DB, Interaction |
| Door and hatch state | the leaf sprite | Layout (PROJECTOR) |
| Gauges and instruments reading authored systems | the in-world instruments and the inspect card, corruptible and falsifiable | In-World Instruments & Inspect View (K-4); placement is Authoring's (DA-34, IW-31) |
| The exterior view of a wreck and its `visible_unknown` | the transit view | Web Map & Transit (undesigned) |
| Save, death restart | the pause panel and the failure stub | Save/Load |
| The content lint report | **plain text in the developer console and CI log**; not a player surface and not part of a release build's UI | Authoring (DA-40, DA-41) |

There is therefore no UX Flag for this system and no `/ux-design` pass is required for it. Two constraints are handed to the owners above, both as content rules this GDD enforces by lint: in MVP no authored piece, template or lint message may carry text, a label, an icon or an arrow (VA-13, AC-DA-53; VA-13 targets UI-like markers, not future diegetic lore fragments, which are the narrative-director's), and no authored record may carry a field whose only use is to be drawn on a HUD. A UI that wants to display more about a wreck has to find it in the world first (P1).

- **Routed ask ASK-F&T-H1 (Failure & Trail and 2D Lighting; CD-GDD-ALIGN concern, applied; nothing here edits either GDD).** `b_reactor`'s HOT shimmer is a standing tell from `T0` 42000 mC, so without a change it looks the same when the room crosses `HEAT_DMG_ONSET_MC` 45000 and starts to damage (P2: nothing escalates). Ask: provide one visible *step* in the room-level heat presentation (a band change in shimmer amplitude or rate; a hue step is allowed only inside the sane palette and never into the flesh band, hue 5 to 30) at `HEAT_TELL_STEP_MC` (Authoring request, 44,700 mC as authored, range 44,000 to 44,800), so the step lands at least `W_MIN_WARNING_S` x 10 = 100 Graph steps before `t_onset_steps` (D.18; 293 steps as authored). F&T's heat shimmer today is a sev-2 stepped strip at the fitting (FT:942, FT:1087, FT-24) and Lighting's heat read has no onset band; which of the two owns the step is theirs to answer (OQ-DA-37). The fix is not a marker: "told by the shimmer, never by a marker" (DA-28) is unchanged.

## Acceptance Criteria

*Every criterion is pass/fail from this section plus the glossary below. **Tags:** **(auto)** a headless test under `tests/integration/derelict_authoring/` or `tests/unit/derelict_authoring/` using the kernel's headless path (H10, no scene tree, no window); **(lint)** the CONTENT lint of DA-40, offline and in CI (BOOT rules are marked); **(review)** a code or document review with a retained record (reviewer, date, hits found); **(manual)** an instrumented play or lit capture with retained evidence in `production/qa/evidence/derelict-authoring/`; **(spike)** a pass/fail number from a time-boxed ENGINE spike. Modifiers: **(ASSET)** run by `tools/asset-pipeline/` over sheets, not templates; **(perf)** a timing measurement; **(integration)** needs more than one system running. Bracketed ids name the Core Rule (`DA-n`), Formula (`D.n`), edge case or owner criterion a line discharges. Playtest numbers marked `[PLACEHOLDER: creative-director]` stay placeholders until a second round of 8 or more testers. **`tests/` and the gdUnit4 addon do not exist yet (Item DB OQ 28): every (auto) and (lint) line is a specification until the project's test-setup story lands, and none may be reported as passed before then.** Criteria blocked on an open ask say BLOCKED-ON and cite it; a blocked criterion is never marked passed by this GDD. Story-type mapping: lint lines are Config/Data (treated as BLOCKING in CI regardless of `testing.strict.config`, because DA-41 makes every ERROR a merge block); lines needing the Graph to run are Integration (BLOCKING); captures are Visual/Feel (BLOCKING, retained screenshot plus lead sign-off, not waived at `qa.level: minimal`).*

### Glossary of names used below

| Name | Meaning |
|---|---|
| V1, V3, V5, V8, V9, V12 | verbs: V1 patch a fitting; V3 close a breaker (restore power); V5 open or close a valve; V8 cut or salvage a module; V9 weld; V12 hand-crank a SCRAMmed reactor (M11) |
| fault ids | the eight live faults by `fault_id`: `CLT-LEAK` (`p_cryo_rad`), `O2-SHIP` (`d_cryo_exposed`), `PWR-OVL` (`cab_trunk`), `FUEL-DRIP` (`pf_feed`) on `home_ship`; broken breaker (`j_a_main`) and pump stall (`pump_a`) on A; `O2-ROOM` (the weld seam) and `HULL-FATIGUE` (`seg_b_pump_n`) on B (DA-15) |
| literal / gated reading | of Graph D.9: *literal* = a leak-mode subject loses `hp` while in mode LEAK even at `intensity_pm` 0; *gated* = wear only while `intensity_pm` > 0 (ASK-G1); gated binds since 2026-10-27 |
| LSG D.15 / D.15 | "LSG D.15" is the Graph's fault-fixture table; "D.15" alone is this GDD's patch and fatigue treadmill |
| ASK-G1 | ask to the Graph owner: gate leak wear on `intensity_pm` > 0; **ANSWERED 2026-10-27** in LS-11 and LSG D.9 (LSG:127, 515, 762), so the closed-valve staging ships (OQ-DA-18) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* |
| ASK-G3 | the scrubber's `R_SCR`, 200 in one place and 400 in another: answered by inspection, 400 (IDB:212, LSG D.5); the 200 is the severed-port leak floor (IDB:705); the owners confirm (OQ-DA-25) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* |
| ASK-I1 | ask to Interaction: register subjects by body adjacency or proximity, not by "loaded" (OQ-DA-20) |
| OQ-DA-n, OQ-V*n* | Open Questions rows of this GDD (the table at the end); OQ-V rows are the art-director's |
| `S0` to `S4` | the five reachable loaded sets: S0 `{home}`, S1 `{home, A}`, S2 `{home, B}`, S3 `{home+B}`, S4 `{home+B, A}` (DA-32) |
| art-side checks | the art-director's twelve are carried as AC-DA-07, 33, 37, 38, 49, 50, 51, 52, 55, 93, 125 and 126 (see Visual/Audio Requirements, last sub-sections) |

The build also protects P2 at the fairness floor: `PUMP_TELL_UNDER_FAIRNESS` (AC-DA-134) ERRORs below 100 steps even if the 900-step target slips, so drift is caught at the lint and not discovered in play. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* [D.16]

### Experience targets (playtest-checkable)

Each round is five testers, at least three of whom are outside the project (the concept's kill/continue gate). The targets are endorsed (creative-director ruling); only the thresholds are `[PLACEHOLDER: creative-director]`. Five testers and 4-of-5 thresholds are a smoke signal: a second round of 8 or more is required before any threshold is promoted `[PLACEHOLDER: creative-director]`. Each run keeps its scoring sheet, observer log and sign-off in `production/qa/evidence/derelict-authoring/`.

- **AC-DA-87 (manual)** X-DA-1: GIVEN five first-time testers boarding derelict A with a stopwatch started when `a_dock` loads, WHEN asked within 60 s, THEN at least 4 of 5 name the failed loop **and point at the specific dried trail that told them** (the frost fan at `pa_frozen` or the radial scorch at `j_a_main`); counted correct only when the named loop matches the trail pointed at (cold, frozen or coolant with the frost fan; electrical, power or short with the scorch), so a guess that names both loops without a trail does not pass *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*.
- **AC-DA-88 (manual)** X-DA-2: GIVEN five testers in transit, WHEN the wreck appears, THEN at least 4 of 5 point at the residual light, and at least 4 of 5 are standing in the lamp's host room (`a_life` on A, `b_breach` on B) within 3 minutes of boarding without a marker.
- **AC-DA-89 (manual)** X-DA-3: GIVEN five testers at A's dead branch, WHEN they reach the breaker decision, THEN at least 3 of 5 pause or voice a cost; the observer records which. A **pause** counts only when no movement input for at least 3 s with the breaker in reach is coded together with gaze at the breaker, the branch or the lamps (camera or cursor dwell on them) or an utterance in that window, so an idle stop does not count *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*. A **voiced cost** is coded when, within 10 s before the decision, the tester names a consequence of waking in their own words (a clock or failure, noise or being seen, light the web can see, power or battery drain), whether or not they then act; the observer logs the utterance verbatim with its timestamp and a second rater codes it blind from the log ("will this make noise?" counts, "what does this do?" does not) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*.
- **AC-DA-90 (manual)** X-DA-4: GIVEN five testers in the cryo bay, WHEN observed, THEN none is lost (no movement input and no interaction) for more than 30 s and at least 4 of 5 enter the trail-to-fitting cells before any other exit. The far-wall frost needs `p_cryo_rad` `fail_since_step` -60 (DA-15 #1), accepted by M1 (OQ-DA-1 is answered).
- **AC-DA-91 (manual)** X-DA-5: GIVEN five testers (at least three outside the project) after one weld and one session, WHEN each is asked to walk to the seam and say which side was the original ship without a prompt, THEN at least 4 of 5 `[PLACEHOLDER: creative-director]` name the maintained side, each scored right or wrong against the template's `kind` (Layout X-1; the blind-capture form is AC-DA-07) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*.
- **AC-DA-92 (manual)** X-DA-6: GIVEN five testers after a session, WHEN asked the neutral prompt "tell me about what you took and what you left" (the earlier "could you have taken more?" led the answer) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*, THEN at least 3 of 5 answer about choice or risk, classified by a written codebook by two raters with a third resolving disagreements: an answer codes CHOICE or RISK if it names a trade-off, a risk or a reason ("I would have lost X", "it was loud", "I could carry only one", "it wasn't worth it") and INVENTORY if it names only a count, space or missing items ("I ran out of room", "there was more loot", "I didn't find the rest"); a mixed answer codes by its first clause; the coded transcript is retained *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*.
- **AC-DA-93 (manual)** GIVEN five testers who entered derelict A, WHEN scored, THEN (a) each names after leaving the loop that failed (coolant or power) and the cold, scored against the fault table, and answers the three ART 6.0 questions from the world alone (who built it, what was done to it, has the web come in), at least 4 of 5, each scored by two raters against this key: *who built it* is right if the tester says it was made by the same kind of people as the home-ship or names Foundry cues (round ports, big rivets, squat panels); *what was done to it* is right if they name one observed repair, missing module, patch, dried trail or cut; *has the web come in* is right if they say no for A; a tester passes when all three are right *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*; (b) a still of each door at three tiles in greyscale, open versus closed, is identified correctly by at least 4 of 5 (X-DA-7); and (c) asked afterwards whether the lamp coming on at home was something they did or something that happened, at least 4 of 5 say they did it. [Game Feel; Visual/Audio]
- **AC-DA-135 (manual)** X-DA-8: GIVEN five testers (at least three outside the project) who each reach A's dead branch, WHEN the observer logs each tester's outcome from the world state (the branch's lanterns lit and `j_a_main` swapped = wake; the branch still dark with the failed `scr_a` cut by beam alone = leave-it-dark), THEN across the five both outcomes occur at least once, and every tester logged leave-it-dark took the cut by beam and, asked at the debrief, can say why they carried the scrubber home or left it; if only one outcome occurs in five, the result is "restraint is not reading as a real choice" and is reported to the creative-director, not averaged away. [P3; Player Fantasy anchor moment] User ruling D45 (2026-10-27): the reasons coded here are the trigger for any authored payoff of waking the branch (OQ-DA-43 options B to D); if most testers who woke it give no reason beyond the D.22 window, or most who left it dark say the window was not worth it, report that to the creative-director with the log *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*. **Coding rule** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*: a wake whose stated reason is curiosity only ("to see what happens", "it was there") is logged but does not count toward the "both outcomes occur" pass, because by D.22 every in-room act fits the cold-room budget without heat and the window buys unhurried time, not a new act; **pre-wired trigger:** if fewer than 2 of 5 testers wake the branch for a non-curiosity reason, the creative-director's payoff decision (OQ-DA-43 options B to D) is scheduled for the next content pass, not left to be rediscovered.
- **AC-DA-136 (manual)** X-DA-9: GIVEN five testers (at least three outside the project) playing the first 45 minutes of the MVP without being told the treadmill is a tuning concern, WHEN each is asked, unprompted by wording that names it, how re-patching the home leaks felt, THEN fewer than 2 of 5 `[PLACEHOLDER: creative-director]` describe it as tedious or a chore (classified by the AC-DA-92 codebook, with TEDIOUS or CHORE coded from the words "tedious", "chore", "busywork", "nagging" or "again and again" said about the patching, two raters and a third resolving disagreement), and the observer-timed share of active maintenance (channels plus walking to the leaks) in minutes 10 to 45 is at most 8 % with the plate carried and 16 % without it (D.15: 4.9 to 6.8 % and 12.8 to 14.7 % computed) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*; the observer also logs every return home to a FAILED leak or a SCRAM that ran while the tester was away (D.15 `T_AWAY_SAFE_MIN`), with the tester's words on whether they saw it coming *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*; on failure the report names `PATCH_DECAY_STEPS` and the home leak count as the levers, and the part ledger (800 permille) is left unchanged until the next round. [DA-25, D.15; MVP core hypothesis]
- **AC-DA-137 (manual)** X-DA-10 *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*: GIVEN five first-time testers (at least three outside the project) on their first sortie to derelict B, with no plate carried and the seam not yet welded, WHEN observed from `arrive_b_dock`, THEN at least 4 of 5 `[PLACEHOLDER: creative-director]` restart `r_b` (V12) and land the `pb_leak` patch before the floor crossing (step 1,238 after V12, D.21) and finish the sortie without a SCRAM-recovery prompt, none is lost for more than 30 s, the observer logs for each the order chosen (patch first, or V12 first with the patch's step after V12) and the B timeline beat at which each act fell (D.21 B timeline) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*, and the observer logs for each whether `seg_b_pump_n`'s breach at 5.0 minutes and the draught at `door_b_pump_breach` were read before the player acted; at most 1 of 5 later close the seam breaker onto a dead B. [X-DA-10; D.20, D.21, DA-20]
- **AC-DA-138 (lint, auto)** GIVEN `a_machine`, WHEN `COLD_WORK_BUDGET` runs, THEN `t_work_max` is 25.2 s at `T0`, 13.8 s after 30 minutes unheated and 6.2 s at the equilibrium; the longest single in-room act (the `htr_a` cut, 240 ticks = 4.0 s) fits all three, a PATCH (3.0 s) fits all three, a two-channel visit (8.0 s) is reported as exceeding the equilibrium figure and fitting the other two, and Boarding & Salvage D.4 `margin_pm` for the `htr_a` cut is 250 at `T0` (below 1000). [D.10; BS D.4]
- **AC-DA-139 (auto)** `G0_SEAM_CLOSED_ON_DEAD_B`: GIVEN S3 with `r_b` SCRAMmed, `j_b_main` CLOSED and the seam breaker (the seam CABLE sub-edge's, LS-15) closed at step 0 *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*, WHEN the harness re-opens it at step 700 and applies one zero-part PATCH to `cab_trunk`, THEN no home subject ends FAILED or DESTROYED; WHEN it re-opens at step 900, THEN `cab_trunk` is FAILED (home blackout) and a zero-part PATCH after the breaker is open restores it to `PATCH_CAP_BP`; WHEN it re-opens at step 1,112 or later, THEN `cab_trunk` is DESTROYED and `NOT_REPAIRABLE` and the run records the one residual run-ender (the remedy is the last autosave). - **AC-DA-97 (auto)** GIVEN each of the three ship templates built through the real pipeline, WHEN every node, edge, door, fixture and loose item initial value is read back and diffed against `default_state()`, THEN the diff lists exactly the rows of the DA-35 table and nothing else (home: `j_cryo` `breaker_closed` false; `fx_corr_1` and `fx_corr_2` `base_pm` 750 *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review, delegated ruling R7)*; `v_o2_cryo` `open` false (the closed-valve staging, DA-16); `v_fuel_feed` and `v_clt_isolate` `open` true; `ot_main` 3,000,000 mGU; `res_main` 20,000 mL; `b_main` 2,160,000 dJ; `door_engine_n` OPEN and every other door CLOSED; `bcn_main` 0 W requested; A: `j_a_main` `hp` 2000 and `breaker_closed` false, `b_a1` 540,000, `b_a2` 1,620,000; B: `r_b` SCRAMmed (`j_b_main` is absent from the diff: CLOSED by default, DA-20 *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*), `res_b` 16,000, `b_b1` 432,000, `seg_b_pump_n` 6999 `fatigued`, `seg_b_breach` 0; and `p_cryo_rad` `fail_since_step` -60 on `home_ship`); and changing one authored value by 1 (for example `b_main` `stock` by 1 dJ) changes both `template_hash` and `state_hash`. [DA-35, DA-44; LY-26]

### Gaps / untestable as written

The criteria above are complete as specifications; the following cannot be marked passed by this GDD, and the list is the QA position of record.

- **BLOCKED-ON owners:** none remain on OQ-DA-1 or ASK-G1 (answered in the Graph text on 2026-10-27; AC-DA-19, 21, 22, 24, 59, 75, 90, 99 and 124 verify the answers and revert to BLOCKED if the cited lines change *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*); AC-DA-33 and AC-DA-34 (Emitter placeholders); AC-DA-41 (Graph owner confirms the `a_machine` equilibrium); AC-DA-42 (Suit placeholders); AC-DA-46 (Lighting's `CONTAIN_COLOURS`); AC-DA-47 (ASK-I1 or the joint ask); AC-DA-114 (OQ-DA-11); AC-DA-115 (OQ-DA-17); AC-DA-116 (OQ-DA-19); AC-DA-130 (OQ-DA-30); AC-DA-132 is no longer blocked (OQ-DA-34 answered by LSG D.6 Example D, re-review) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*; AC-DA-98 and AC-DA-99 (`init_world` and the Save/Load harness).
- **Depend on unbuilt or undesigned systems:** AC-DA-11, AC-DA-15 (O2-ROOM seam instance), AC-DA-23, AC-DA-83 and AC-DA-85 need a weld and Modular Ship Assembly (on disk, In Design) has no build yet, so the harness drives the Graph's `weld_join` and Layout's LM2 `attach_section` directly (standing in for Assembly's atomic apply) with a stated weld quality `q`; AC-DA-38 needs Web Map & Transit and the Perception Filter.
- **Release-blocking position (qa-lead):** AC-DA-22 is release-blocking for MVP and runs on one staging: the closed-valve staging ships because the Graph accepted ASK-G1 (2026-10-27); the valve-OPEN contingency applies only if the cited Graph text reverts (DA-16) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*.
- **Thin margin:** AC-DA-77 passes at 1,012 steps against the 900-step target (112 steps of margin, no longer zero); drift of more than 112 steps in a `[UNV: Graph]` assumption fails that target (recovery in the criterion) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*. The fairness floor is separate and guarded by the build: `PUMP_TELL_UNDER_FAIRNESS` (AC-DA-134) ERRORs below 100 steps, so P2 does not depend on the arithmetic.
- **User-ruling additions (2026-10-27):** AC-DA-144 is BLOCKED-ON Layout's CONSOLE anchor with `console_role` only (the Emitter's reach stands at 48 px, G1); AC-DA-145 waits for the art-director's ratio; AC-DA-148 is `(provisional)` BLOCKED until the spike fixes the carry geometry (OQ-DA-45) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*.
- **Cross-GDD additions (2026-10-28):** AC-DA-160 is `(provisional)` BLOCKED until the spike fixes the carry geometry (OQ-DA-45, with the reach's fixture and stick-shelf choice, OQ-DA-57); AC-DA-162 is BLOCKED-ON the content plan adding dwell sources (OQ-DA-56): the current estimate of 104 s fails the 190 s line by design of the budget, not by a rule defect; AC-DA-159's `j_a_main` mode (6 STARVE or 10 WORN) follows the Graph's capability wording for mode 6 (`[UNV: Graph]`) *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)*.
- **Re-review additions:** AC-DA-150's FAILED branch and AC-DA-139's depend on the Graph's short-burn answer (OQ-DA-53); AC-DA-152 needs the flag's schema owner (OQ-DA-49); AC-DA-24 (b) carries an `[UNV: Graph D.8]` window for the re-SCRAM step; AC-DA-153 runs on a Suit stub until the Suit build exists *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*.
- **Playtest numbers** (AC-DA-59, 87 to 93, 135 to 137, 151 *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*) are smoke signals on five testers and stay `[PLACEHOLDER: creative-director]` until a round of 8 or more.
- **Not testable by this GDD at all:** the Perception Filter's behaviour on authored content (undesigned; this includes the Filter half of AC-DA-38, NOT ASSESSED); whether the engine's JSON loader, export filter and editor tooling behave as assumed (`[UNV: engine]`, AC-DA-60); the frame budget (the Pipeline's and Lighting's).
- **Naming and layout of the test files** follow `.claude/rules/test-standards.md`, which this GDD did not re-read; the test-setup story confirms the file names.

## Open Questions

Owner is `user` (a scope, fiction or sign-off decision only the user can make), `agent` (a named specialist or the owner GDD's author can answer) or `GDD` (this document resolves it on the next revision). Target is `playtest`, `next GDD` (the owner GDD's next revision or a downstream GDD) or `ADR`. Rows cited in the body carry their id there; the rest are routed asks. **BLOCKING** means an MVP acceptance criterion cannot pass while it is open. User-decision items come first.

**Closed by the user rulings (2026-10-27).** OQ-DA-43 (D45, option A), OQ-DA-20 (D37, adjacency registration), OQ-DA-30 (D47, option (b)) and OQ-DA-41 (b) (D17, the weld autosave) are closed or answered as user rulings; OQ-DA-39's console-light clause is answered by DA-46 (X2) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*; its CONSOLE-anchor ask is resolved on this side by delegated ruling G6 (2026-10-28).

**Closed in this run (kept for the record).** OQ-DA-3, R7's charge arithmetic: replaced by loaded-time clocks (15 % and 12 % of `BATT_CELL`; A 45 and B 36 minutes) and accepted at the advisor checkpoint. OQ-DA-9, may the beacon share a node with a fixture: **no**, `bcn_main` is a dedicated LOAD node, because `set_beacon` off issues `set_load_request(node_ref, 0)` and would zero a shared lamp while its light stayed lit (P1). The FUEL-DRIP lever and witness (Checkpoint Q1), the wake-light scope (Q2), the `_worn` vocabulary (Q3), the pod-stub ownership split (Q6), `COLD_ROOM_SAFE_MIN` 25 (Q7) and `pb_leak` 2900 (Q8) were ruled at the checkpoint and are in the body.
